# Phase 3b: The Layered-Sphere Kernel — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use usask-foundation:subagent-driven-development to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Layered spheres (a core and any number of concentric shells), with every output of a sphere and derivatives with respect to every layer:
- validated against independent Arb references and dense accuracy maps, with a declared validated domain;
- available from Rust (`scatrs::layered`) and Python (`scatrs.layered`);
- compared with scattnlay;
- documented.

**Architecture:**
- **Kernel.** `scatrs::layered::Kernel<T>` (generic over `Real`) computes, for each order, the effective logarithmic derivatives Hₐ and H_b at the outer surface of every layer, from the core out. It then calls the sphere's outer step (`mie::kernel::State::finish`) with them, either as δ = H − D_n(m x_L) or as H itself, so that the sphere's cancellation-free numerators and absorption sum carry over.
- **Outputs.** The outputs come from 3a's shared `Coefficients` view. Each layer is a derivative `Group`, whose `outer` flag marks the layer that scales the outputs.
- **Derivatives.** They are dual numbers through the generic kernel, seeded in every layer's x and Re m. One layer delegates to `Mie` and is bitwise a sphere.
- **References.** A new Arb generator matches the fields at each interface directly, without the kernel's recursion.

**Tech Stack:**
- Rust 2024 (MSRV 1.85; CI lints with 1.99), pyo3/numpy 0.29.
- Python ≥ 3.11 and python-flint (references).
- criterion, scattnlay 2.4 (benchmarks only), Sphinx 9 + myst-nb.

**Spec:** `docs/specs/2026-10-14-phase-3-layered-spheres-design.md`, sections 1.2, 2, 4, 5.1, 6–11, 15. What the implementation changed, and why, is in its section 16; the deferred items are in `docs/plans/phase-3b-followups.md`.

## Global Constraints

- **Spheres stay bitwise.** Every output of `scatrs::mie` and of the ensembles is bitwise unchanged: the 3a Rust snapshot (`docs/plans/2026-10-14-phase-3a-refactor.md`, appendix A) after every task.
- **One layer, and layers of one index, give the homogeneous sphere's results bitwise.**
- **Declared accuracy.** Every output inside the declared domain meets its tolerance at every Arb reference and on both accuracy maps. Each declared constant is at least 4 times the worst case found.
- **Checks at every commit:**
  - `cargo fmt --all`;
  - `cargo +1.99.0 clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` (stop on any output, not just on the exit status);
  - `cargo test --workspace`;
  - `RUSTDOCFLAGS="-D warnings" cargo doc -p scatrs --no-deps`;
  - `uv run pytest -q`, ruff check and ruff format;
  - `uv run python -m mypy.stubtest scatrs._scatrs`;
  - `uv run --directory reference python -m pytest -q`;
  - from task 6, the docs build `sphinx-build -E -W --keep-going`.
- **Docs are user-facing:** no phases or development history.
- Commits end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. Never send personal data in network requests.

## How this plan was made

As for 3a, every task was prototyped and verified before this plan was written. The diffs below are the commits on `phase-3`, without generated data:
- `reference/data/*.jsonl` and the manifest, regenerated by `python -m scatrs_reference generate --only layered layered_derivatives`;
- `docs/validation/data/layered-*.json`, written by the maps with `SCATRS_ACCURACY_MAP`;
- `benchmarks/results/*-scattnlay.json`, written by the script;
- `benchmarks/uv.lock`, from `uv lock`.

The tasks are in their prototype order. Task 2 adds the references, and with them the four accuracy fixes the references forced, so its diff changes task 1's code. An executor may fold those fixes into task 1. Task 3's maps forced the domain and constants it declares.

| What | Measured |
|---|---|
| mpmath, 60 digits: Yang's recursion, the pole-free form and the δ split against direct interface matching | agree to 10⁻⁴⁰ or better (3 particles, orders 1–9) |
| Q_abs, f64 against double-double, two layers, k = 10⁻² … 10⁻¹², x = 10⁻³ … 100 | ξ only: up to 1.1·10⁻⁵ (k = 10⁻¹², x = 100); χ for thin layers: ≤ 1.6·10⁻¹⁵ for x ≤ 10, ≤ 4·10⁻¹³ at x = 100 |
| Arb references (`layered_reference.rs`): 362 value cases and 8 derivative cases in the domain | worst C: Q_ext 6.0 / 32, Q_abs 6.0 / 128, S 3.1 / 128, coefficient derivatives 2.0 / 1024; double-double within its slack |
| Accuracy map (`layered_accuracy_map.rs`): 101 x_L × 51 particles, 12.6 s | worst C: coefficients 5.9 / 32, Q_ext 6.6 / 32, Q_abs 21 / 128, Q_back 12.6 / 64, amplitudes 12.9 / 128, coefficient derivatives 164 / 1024, Jacobian 35 / 256, amplitude derivatives 205 / 1024 |
| Greek map (`layered_greek_accuracy_map.rs`): 46 x_L × 21 particles, 8 s | worst C: phase matrix 8.8 / 64, Greek 2.4 / 16, their derivatives 56 / 256 and 50 / 256 |
| Before the domain limits | coefficients up to 10⁹ ulps at x_L = 10⁴, m = 10 thin shell (|m|x = 10⁵); amplitude derivatives C ≈ 6000 for x_L ~ 10³ |
| scattnlay comparison, 362 cases | scatrs ≤ 9.3·10⁻¹¹; scattnlay > 10⁻¹⁰ in 100–174, > 10⁻⁶ in 46–71, failed 6–9; scatrs 4.3–8.4× faster |
| criterion (`--bench layered`), one thread | two layers 2.5–2.65× a sphere; derivatives 4.6–7.4× (two layers), 5.6–8.9× (three), 25–32× (ten) |
| Tests | Rust 361 → 377 passed, 3 ignored; Python 384 → 394, 1 skipped; reference 146 → 148 |

**Interfaces:**
- **Produces (public):** `scatrs::layered::{Layered, LayeredSolution, MAX_LAYERS, VALIDATED_DOMAIN, ValidatedDomain}`.
  - `Layered::compute(&[T], &[Complex<T>], &MieOptions)` and `compute_with_derivatives`.

- **Produces (crate-internal):**
  - `mie::kernel::{Side, Outer, ratios, ratios_real, ratios_complex}`;
  - `State::{prepare, u_z, finish}`;
  - `complex::exp_m1`;
  - `Group::outer`.

diff --git a/scatrs/src/complex.rs b/scatrs/src/complex.rs
index 867803e..2e8cd84 100644
--- a/scatrs/src/complex.rs
+++ b/scatrs/src/complex.rs
@@ -26,11 +26,46 @@ pub(crate) fn inv<T: Real>(z: Complex<T>) -> Complex<T> {
     }
 }
 
+/// e^w − 1 for Re w ≤ 0, accurate to a few ulps of |e^w − 1| also where |w| is small:
+/// (e^a cos b − 1) + i e^a sin b for w = a + ib, with e^a cos b − 1 = expm1(a) cos b −
+/// 2 sin²(b/2), whose two terms have the same sign wherever they are small (a ≤ 0).
+#[inline]
+pub(crate) fn exp_m1<T: Real>(w: Complex<T>) -> Complex<T> {
+    let (a, b) = (w.re, w.im);
+    let (s, c) = b.sin_cos();
+    let h = (b * 0.5).sin();
+    Complex::new(a.exp_m1() * c - h * h * 2.0, a.exp() * s)
+}
+
 #[cfg(test)]
 mod tests {
     use super::*;
     use crate::scalar::DoubleDouble as Dd;
 
+    /// `exp_m1` in f64 against the same formula in double-double (whose `exp_m1`, `sin` and
+    /// `cos` are checked against Arb), from |w| = 10⁻²⁰ to 40, Re w ≤ 0: within 4 ulps of
+    /// |e^w − 1|; and against the series w + w²/2 at |w| = 10⁻¹².
+    #[test]
+    fn exp_m1_is_accurate_for_small_and_large_arguments() {
+        let mut worst = 0.0f64;
+        for i in 0..400 {
+            let r = 10f64.powf(-20.0 + 21.6 * f64::from(i) / 399.0);
+            let t = core::f64::consts::PI * (0.5 + f64::from(i % 37) / 36.0);
+            let w = c(r * t.cos().min(0.0), r * t.sin());
+            let got = exp_m1(w);
+            let wd = Complex::new(Dd::from(w.re), Dd::from(w.im));
+            let want = exp_m1(wd);
+            let want = c(want.re.hi(), want.im.hi());
+            let err = (got - want).norm() / want.norm();
+            worst = worst.max(err);
+            assert!(err <= 4.0 * f64::EPSILON, "w = {w}: error {err:e}");
+        }
+        let w = c(-3e-13, 4e-13);
+        let series = w + w * w * 0.5;
+        assert!(rel(exp_m1(w), series) <= 2.0 * f64::EPSILON);
+        eprintln!("exp_m1: worst error {:.2} ulps", worst / f64::EPSILON);
+    }
+
     fn c(re: f64, im: f64) -> Complex<f64> {
         Complex::new(re, im)
     }
diff --git a/scatrs/src/layered/domain.rs b/scatrs/src/layered/domain.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b9a9e5a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scatrs/src/layered/domain.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,112 @@
+//! The validated domain of layered spheres and their declared accuracy.
+
+use core::fmt;
+
+use num_complex::Complex;
+
+use crate::mie::MieOutput;
+
+/// The region of inputs whose accuracy has been validated, and the declared tolerances there.
+///
+/// Inside it, every output of [`Layered::compute`](super::Layered::compute) in `f64`, with the
+/// default truncation or more terms, and of its solution's methods, is within
+///
+/// C·(κ′(x, m) + cond)·2⁻⁵²
+///
+/// of the exact value for the exact inputs, in the measure of its [`MieOutput`], with cond the
+/// output's relative condition number with respect to every layer's x_l, Re m_l and Im m_l
+/// (as [`mie::ValidatedDomain`](crate::mie::ValidatedDomain) defines it for one layer) and κ′
+/// the a-priori factor of [`condition_factor`](Self::condition_factor). The constants are
+/// measured, at least 4 times the worst case found at every Arb reference value in the domain
+/// and against double-double on a dense grid (the accuracy map).
+#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
+#[non_exhaustive]
+pub struct ValidatedDomain {
+    /// The smallest validated size parameter of the outer surface.
+    pub x_min: f64,
+    /// The largest validated size parameter of the outer surface.
+    pub x_max: f64,
+    /// The largest validated Re m of any layer (the smallest is any value above 0).
+    pub m_re_max: f64,
+    /// The largest validated Im m of any layer (the smallest is 0).
+    pub m_im_max: f64,
+    /// The smallest validated |m| of any layer.
+    pub abs_m_min: f64,
+    /// The largest validated number of layers.
+    pub max_layers: usize,
+}
+
+/// The validated domain: x_L ∈ [10⁻⁶, 10⁴], every layer's m with 0 < Re m ≤ 10, 0 ≤ Im m ≤ 10
+/// and |m| ≥ 0.1, at most 16 layers, any radii.
+pub const VALIDATED_DOMAIN: ValidatedDomain = ValidatedDomain {
+    x_min: 1e-6,
+    x_max: 1e4,
+    m_re_max: 10.0,
+    m_im_max: 10.0,
+    abs_m_min: 0.1,
+    max_layers: 16,
+};
+
+impl ValidatedDomain {
+    /// Whether the layered sphere of interface size parameters `x` (the outer one last) and
+    /// indices `m` is inside the domain.
+    pub fn contains(&self, x: &[f64], m: &[Complex<f64>]) -> bool {
+        let Some(&outer) = x.last() else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        x.len() <= self.max_layers
+            && (self.x_min..=self.x_max).contains(&outer)
+            && m.iter().all(|m| {
+                m.re > 0.0
+                    && m.re <= self.m_re_max
+                    && (0.0..=self.m_im_max).contains(&m.im)
+                    && m.norm() >= self.abs_m_min
+            })
+    }
+
+    /// κ(x_L, m) = (1 + max_l max(1, |m_l|)·x_L)·max(1, max_l |m_l| / max_l |m_l − 1|): as for
+    /// a sphere, with the largest index and the largest contrast of any layer. Infinite when
+    /// every layer has m = 1.
+    pub fn condition_factor(x: f64, m: &[Complex<f64>]) -> f64 {
+        let big = m.iter().map(|m| m.norm()).fold(1.0, f64::max);
+        let contrast = m
+            .iter()
+            .map(|m| (m - Complex::new(1.0, 0.0)).norm())
+            .fold(0.0, f64::max);
+        (1.0 + big * x) * (big / contrast).max(1.0)
+    }
+
+    /// The declared bound on the error of `output`: C·(κ′ + `condition`)·2⁻⁵², with κ′ as for
+    /// a sphere ([`mie::ValidatedDomain::tolerance`](crate::mie::ValidatedDomain::tolerance))
+    /// with [`condition_factor`](Self::condition_factor) for κ.
+    pub fn tolerance(&self, output: MieOutput, x: f64, m: &[Complex<f64>], condition: f64) -> f64 {
+        let kappa = Self::condition_factor(x, m);
+        let big = m.iter().map(|m| m.norm()).fold(1.0, f64::max);
+        let near_one = (kappa / (1.0 + big * x)).max(1.0);
+        let quadrature = |order: usize| ((order as f64 + 1.0).powi(2) + x * x) / 8.0;
+        let prior = match output {
+            MieOutput::CoefficientDerivatives | MieOutput::AmplitudeDerivatives => 1.0 + big * x,
+            MieOutput::EfficiencyJacobian | MieOutput::PhaseMatrixDerivatives => kappa * near_one,
+            MieOutput::GreekCoefficients { order } => kappa + quadrature(order),
+            MieOutput::GreekDerivatives { order } => kappa * near_one + quadrature(order),
+            _ => kappa,
+        };
+        Self::constant(output) * (prior + condition) * f64::EPSILON
+    }
+
+    /// The constant C of [`tolerance`](Self::tolerance).
+    pub fn constant(output: MieOutput) -> f64 {
+        crate::mie::ValidatedDomain::constant(output)
+    }
+}
+
+impl fmt::Display for ValidatedDomain {
+    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
+        write!(
+            f,
+            "x_L in [{:e}, {:e}], every layer 0 < Re m <= {}, 0 <= Im m <= {}, |m| >= {}, at \
+             most {} layers",
+            self.x_min, self.x_max, self.m_re_max, self.m_im_max, self.abs_m_min, self.max_layers
+        )
+    }
+}
diff --git a/scatrs/src/layered/kernel.rs b/scatrs/src/layered/kernel.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1c7ec2d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scatrs/src/layered/kernel.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,202 @@
+//! The layered-sphere recursion: the effective log-derivatives Hₐ and H_b at the outer surface of
+//! each layer, from the core outward (Yang 2003; Peña & Pal 2009), in the pole-free form of the
+//! theory chapter, then the homogeneous outer step of [`crate::mie`] with them.
+//!
+//! Notation (layers l = 0, …, L − 1 from the core; z₁ = m_l x_{l−1} and z₂ = m_l x_l for
+//! l ≥ 1): u_n = ψ_n/ψ_{n−1}, D⁽¹⁾_n = (n + 1)/z − u_{n+1}, w_n = ξ_n/ξ_{n−1},
+//! D⁽³⁾_n = (n + 1)/z − w_{n+1}, Π_n = ψ_n ξ_n, P_n = [ψ_n/ξ_n](z₁)·ξ_n(z₂)², and for layer l
+//! G₁ = m_l H − m_{l−1} D⁽¹⁾_n(z₁), G₂ = m_l H − m_{l−1} D⁽³⁾_n(z₁) for Hₐ (m_l and m_{l−1}
+//! exchanged for H_b), with
+//!
+//! H = D⁽³⁾_n(z₂) − i G₂/(Π_n(z₂) G₂ − P_n G₁) = D⁽¹⁾_n(z₂) + δ,
+//! δ = −i P_n G₁/[Π_n(z₂)(Π_n(z₂) G₂ − P_n G₁)].
+
+use num_complex::Complex;
+
+use crate::complex::{exp_m1, inv};
+use crate::mie::kernel::{Outer, Side, State, int, ratios, real};
+use crate::scalar::Real;
+
+/// Π_n(z) and w_{n+1}(z) of one argument, advanced order by order from Π₀ = (1 − e^(2iz))/2
+/// and w₁ = 1/z − i by Π_n = Π_{n−1} u_n w_n and w_{n+1} = (2n + 1)/z − 1/w_n (the upward ξ
+/// recurrence, stable for Im z ≥ 0).
+#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
+struct Arg<T> {
+    inv_z: Complex<T>,
+    pi: Complex<T>,
+    /// w_{n+1} after `step(n)`.
+    w: Complex<T>,
+    /// w_n after `step(n)`.
+    w_n: Complex<T>,
+}
+
+/// 2iz.
+fn two_i<T: Real>(z: Complex<T>) -> Complex<T> {
+    Complex::new(-(z.im * 2.0), z.re * 2.0)
+}
+
+impl<T: Real> Arg<T> {
+    fn new(z: Complex<T>) -> Self {
+        let inv_z = inv(z);
+        Self {
+            inv_z,
+            pi: -exp_m1(two_i(z)).scale(T::from_f64(0.5)),
+            w: inv_z - Complex::new(T::zero(), T::one()),
+            w_n: real(T::zero()),
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// From order n − 1 to n, with u_n(z).
+    fn step(&mut self, n: usize, u_n: Complex<T>) {
+        self.w_n = self.w;
+        self.pi = self.pi * u_n * self.w;
+        self.w = self.inv_z.scale(int(2 * n + 1)) - inv(self.w);
+    }
+
+    /// D⁽¹⁾_n(z), with u_{n+1}(z).
+    fn d1(&self, n: usize, u_next: Complex<T>) -> Complex<T> {
+        self.inv_z.scale(int(n + 1)) - u_next
+    }
+
+    /// D⁽³⁾_n(z), after `step(n)`.
+    fn d3(&self, n: usize) -> Complex<T> {
+        self.inv_z.scale(int(n + 1)) - self.w
+    }
+}
+
+/// |re| + |im|.
+fn norm1<T: Real>(z: Complex<T>) -> T {
+    z.re.abs() + z.im.abs()
+}
+
+/// The quantities of one layer's outer step at one order.
+struct Step<T> {
+    d1i: Complex<T>,
+    d3i: Complex<T>,
+    d1o: Complex<T>,
+    d3o: Complex<T>,
+    pio: Complex<T>,
+    p: Complex<T>,
+}
+
+/// H at the outer surface of a layer from H at its inner surface (`h`), with `mi` the layer's
+/// index and `mo` the inner one's (exchanged for H_b): as D⁽¹⁾_n(z₂) + δ unless the two cancel
+/// (by more than a factor 4), near the poles of D⁽¹⁾_n(z₂), where it is H in the pole-free form.
+fn side<T: Real>(
+    mi: Complex<T>,
+    mo: Complex<T>,
+    h: Complex<T>,
+    s: &Step<T>,
+) -> (Side<T>, Complex<T>) {
+    let i1 = Complex::new(T::zero(), T::one());
+    let mh = mi * h;
+    let g1 = mh - mo * s.d1i;
+    let g2 = mh - mo * s.d3i;
+    let inv_den = inv(s.pio * g2 - s.p * g1);
+    let delta = -(i1 * s.p * g1 * inv_den * inv(s.pio));
+    let hd = s.d1o + delta;
+    if norm1(hd) * 4.0 >= norm1(s.d1o).max(norm1(delta)) {
+        (Side::Delta(delta), hd)
+    } else {
+        let hp = s.d3o - i1 * g2 * inv_den;
+        (Side::Direct(hp), hp)
+    }
+}
+
+/// A workspace for the recursion and the outer step.
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub(super) struct Kernel<T: Real> {
+    pub(super) mie: State<T>,
+    /// u_k of the core's argument m₀x₀, then for each layer l ≥ 1 of m_l x_{l−1} and, but for
+    /// the outermost (whose ratios `mie` holds), of m_l x_l.
+    u: Vec<Vec<Complex<T>>>,
+    scratch: Vec<T>,
+    inner: Vec<Arg<T>>,
+    outer_args: Vec<Arg<T>>,
+    p: Vec<Complex<T>>,
+    outer: Vec<Outer<T>>,
+}
+
+impl<T: Real> Kernel<T> {
+    pub(super) fn new() -> Self {
+        Self {
+            mie: State::new(),
+            u: Vec::new(),
+            scratch: Vec::new(),
+            inner: Vec::new(),
+            outer_args: Vec::new(),
+            p: Vec::new(),
+            outer: Vec::new(),
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// a_n, b_n for n = 1, …, `n` and the absorption sum of the layered sphere `x`, `m`
+    /// (validated, L ≥ 2) into `self.mie`.
+    pub(super) fn solve(&mut self, x: &[T], m: &[Complex<T>], n: usize) {
+        let layers = x.len();
+        debug_assert!(layers >= 2 && m.len() == layers);
+        let last = layers - 1;
+        self.mie.prepare(x[last], m[last], n);
+        self.u.resize_with(2 * last, Vec::new);
+        ratios(&mut self.u[0], &mut self.scratch, m[0], x[0], n);
+        for l in 1..layers {
+            ratios(&mut self.u[2 * l - 1], &mut self.scratch, m[l], x[l - 1], n);
+            if l < last {
+                ratios(&mut self.u[2 * l], &mut self.scratch, m[l], x[l], n);
+            }
+        }
+        self.inner.clear();
+        self.outer_args.clear();
+        self.p.clear();
+        for l in 1..layers {
+            let z1 = m[l].scale(x[l - 1]);
+            self.inner.push(Arg::new(z1));
+            self.outer_args.push(Arg::new(m[l].scale(x[l])));
+            // P₀ = e^(2i m_l (x_l − x_{l−1}))·(1 − e^(2iz₁))/2.
+            let dx = x[l] - x[l - 1];
+            let (a, b) = (-(m[l].im * dx * 2.0), m[l].re * dx * 2.0);
+            let (sin_b, cos_b) = b.sin_cos();
+            let e = Complex::new(cos_b, sin_b).scale(a.exp());
+            self.p
+                .push(-(e * exp_m1(two_i(z1))).scale(T::from_f64(0.5)));
+        }
+        let core = Arg::new(m[0].scale(x[0]));
+        self.outer.clear();
+        let u_z = self.mie.u_z();
+        for k in 1..=n {
+            let u_of = |l: usize| -> &[Complex<T>] { if l == last { u_z } else { &self.u[2 * l] } };
+            for l in 1..layers {
+                let (u1, u2) = (&self.u[2 * l - 1], u_of(l));
+                let (ai, ao) = (&mut self.inner[l - 1], &mut self.outer_args[l - 1]);
+                ai.step(k, u1[k]);
+                ao.step(k, u2[k]);
+                self.p[l - 1] = self.p[l - 1] * u1[k] * inv(ai.w_n) * ao.w_n * ao.w_n;
+            }
+            let mut ha = core.d1(k, self.u[0][k + 1]);
+            let mut hb = ha;
+            let mut sides = (Side::Delta(real(T::zero())), Side::Delta(real(T::zero())));
+            for l in 1..layers {
+                let (ai, ao) = (&self.inner[l - 1], &self.outer_args[l - 1]);
+                let s = Step {
+                    d1i: ai.d1(k, self.u[2 * l - 1][k + 1]),
+                    d3i: ai.d3(k),
+                    d1o: ao.d1(k, u_of(l)[k + 1]),
+                    d3o: ao.d3(k),
+                    pio: ao.pi,
+                    p: self.p[l - 1],
+                };
+                let (sa, a) = side(m[l], m[l - 1], ha, &s);
+                let (sb, b) = side(m[l - 1], m[l], hb, &s);
+                (ha, hb, sides) = (a, b, (sa, sb));
+            }
+            self.outer.push(sides);
+        }
+        self.mie
+            .finish(x[last], m[last], n, false, Some(&self.outer));
+        // A lossless particle absorbs nothing; H is real then in exact arithmetic, but its
+        // complex intermediates leave a rounding-level imaginary part.
+        if m.iter().all(|m| m.im.is_zero()) {
+            self.mie.abs_sum = T::zero();
+        }
+    }
+}
diff --git a/scatrs/src/layered/mod.rs b/scatrs/src/layered/mod.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..3cb9dae
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scatrs/src/layered/mod.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,659 @@
+//! Scattering by layered spheres: a core and any number of concentric shells.
+//!
+//! [`Layered`] is a reusable workspace, as [`Mie`](crate::mie::Mie) is: [`Layered::compute`]
+//! (or [`Layered::compute_with_derivatives`]) solves one layered sphere and returns a
+//! [`LayeredSolution`], with the same outputs as a sphere's [`MieSolution`](crate::mie::MieSolution):
+//! the coefficients aₙ, bₙ, the efficiencies, amplitudes, phase matrix and Greek coefficients,
+//! and their derivatives with respect to each layer's size parameter and refractive index.
+//!
+//! ```
+//! use num_complex::Complex;
+//! use scatrs::layered::Layered;
+//! use scatrs::mie::MieOptions;
+//!
+//! // Black carbon coated in sulfate: core and outer size parameters, then their indices.
+//! let x = [0.7, 1.3];
+//! let m = [Complex::new(1.95, 0.79), Complex::new(1.43, 1e-8)];
+//! let mut layered = Layered::<f64>::new();
+//! let particle = layered.compute(&x, &m, &MieOptions::new())?;
+//! let q = particle.efficiencies();
+//! assert!(q.q_abs > 0.0 && (q.q_ext - (q.q_sca + q.q_abs)).abs() <= 1e-15 * q.q_ext);
+//! # Ok::<(), scatrs::Error>(())
+//! ```
+//!
+//! # Conventions
+//!
+//! Those of [`crate::mie`]. Layers are numbered from the core (layer 0) outward: layer l lies
+//! between the radii r_{l−1} and r_l (r₋₁ = 0), `x[l]` = 2πr_l/λ is the size parameter of its
+//! outer surface and `m[l]` its refractive index relative to the medium. The outer size
+//! parameter x_L = `x[L − 1]` normalizes the efficiencies and the phase matrix, and sets the
+//! number of terms.
+//!
+//! # Method
+//!
+//! See the theory chapter of the documentation, "Layered spheres". The effective
+//! log-derivatives Hₐ and H_b at the outer surface of each layer follow from the core outward
+//! (Yang 2003; Peña & Pal 2009) in a form without the poles of D_n(m_l x_l), and the outer step
+//! is the homogeneous sphere's with Hₐ and H_b in place of D_n(mx), keeping its numerators free
+//! of cancellation. A single layer is a sphere: it gives [`crate::mie`]'s results bitwise.
+//! Derivatives are forward-mode [`Dual`] numbers through the generic
+//! kernel (for one layer, the sphere's closed forms).
+//!
+//! # Accuracy
+//!
+//! See [`VALIDATED_DOMAIN`].
+
+mod domain;
+mod kernel;
+
+use num_complex::Complex;
+
+pub use domain::{VALIDATED_DOMAIN, ValidatedDomain};
+
+use crate::mie::{
+    AmplitudeBuffers, AngularTable, CoefficientDerivatives, Coefficients, Efficiencies,
+    EfficiencyJacobian, GreekCoefficients, Group, MAX_MIE_ORDER, MAX_REFRACTIVE_INDEX,
+    MAX_SIZE_PARAMETER, MIN_REFRACTIVE_INDEX, MIN_SIZE_PARAMETER, MieOptions, Normalization,
+    PhaseBuffers, default_terms,
+};
+use crate::scalar::{Dual, Real};
+use crate::{Error, Result};
+
+/// The largest number of layers [`Layered::compute`] accepts, 2¹⁰.
+pub const MAX_LAYERS: usize = 1 << 10;
+
+/// The derivatives of one layer's coefficients and of the absorption sum.
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+struct LayerDerivatives<T> {
+    da_dx: Vec<Complex<T>>,
+    da_dm: Vec<Complex<T>>,
+    db_dx: Vec<Complex<T>>,
+    db_dm: Vec<Complex<T>>,
+    abs_sum: [T; 3],
+}
+
+impl<T: Real> LayerDerivatives<T> {
+    fn new() -> Self {
+        Self {
+            da_dx: Vec::new(),
+            da_dm: Vec::new(),
+            db_dx: Vec::new(),
+            db_dm: Vec::new(),
+            abs_sum: [T::zero(); 3],
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/// A kernel in dual numbers with `N` derivatives, allocated on first use.
+type DualKernel<T, const N: usize> = Option<Box<kernel::Kernel<Dual<T, N>>>>;
+
+/// The kernels in dual numbers of a workspace, one per number of derivatives.
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+struct DualKernels<T: Real> {
+    k3: DualKernel<T, 3>,
+    k6: DualKernel<T, 6>,
+    k9: DualKernel<T, 9>,
+    k12: DualKernel<T, 12>,
+}
+
+/// The kernel with `N` derivatives.
+trait DualSlot<T: Real, const N: usize> {
+    fn kernel(&mut self) -> &mut kernel::Kernel<Dual<T, N>>;
+}
+
+macro_rules! dual_slot {
+    ($n:literal, $field:ident) => {
+        impl<T: Real> DualSlot<T, $n> for DualKernels<T> {
+            fn kernel(&mut self) -> &mut kernel::Kernel<Dual<T, $n>> {
+                self.$field
+                    .get_or_insert_with(|| Box::new(kernel::Kernel::new()))
+            }
+        }
+    };
+}
+dual_slot!(3, k3);
+dual_slot!(6, k6);
+dual_slot!(9, k9);
+dual_slot!(12, k12);
+
+/// A reusable workspace for layered spheres, one per thread.
+///
+/// Its buffers grow to the largest number of terms and layers used and are then reused.
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct Layered<T: Real> {
+    kernel: kernel::Kernel<T>,
+    x: T,
+    a: Vec<Complex<T>>,
+    b: Vec<Complex<T>>,
+    abs_sum: T,
+    derivatives: bool,
+    layers: Vec<LayerDerivatives<T>>,
+    duals: DualKernels<T>,
+}
+
+impl<T: Real> Default for Layered<T> {
+    fn default() -> Self {
+        Self::new()
+    }
+}
+
+impl<T: Real> Layered<T> {
+    /// An empty workspace.
+    pub fn new() -> Self {
+        Self {
+            kernel: kernel::Kernel::new(),
+            x: T::zero(),
+            a: Vec::new(),
+            b: Vec::new(),
+            abs_sum: T::zero(),
+            derivatives: false,
+            layers: Vec::new(),
+            duals: DualKernels {
+                k3: None,
+                k6: None,
+                k9: None,
+                k12: None,
+            },
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Solves the layered sphere of interface size parameters `x` (the core's first, the outer
+    /// surface's last) and layer refractive indices `m`: the coefficients and everything
+    /// derived from them, without derivatives.
+    ///
+    /// # Errors
+    ///
+    /// * [`Error::InvalidInput`] if `x` and `m` are empty or of different lengths, if there are
+    ///   more than [`MAX_LAYERS`] layers, if `x` is not nondecreasing, if x₀ is below
+    ///   [`MIN_SIZE_PARAMETER`] or x_L above [`MAX_SIZE_PARAMETER`], if any m is not finite, has
+    ///   Re m < 0 or Im m < 0, or |m| outside [[`MIN_REFRACTIVE_INDEX`],
+    ///   [`MAX_REFRACTIVE_INDEX`]], or if the number of terms is 0 or above [`MAX_MIE_ORDER`].
+    /// * [`Error::OutsideValidatedDomain`] if the inputs are outside [`VALIDATED_DOMAIN`] or
+    ///   `n_max` is below the default, unless `allow_unvalidated` is set.
+    ///
+    /// For `Dual` inputs the checks apply to the values.
+    pub fn compute(
+        &mut self,
+        x: &[T],
+        m: &[Complex<T>],
+        opts: &MieOptions,
+    ) -> Result<LayeredSolution<'_, T>> {
+        let n = check_inputs(x, m, opts)?;
+        let last = x.len() - 1;
+        if last == 0 {
+            self.kernel.mie.solve(x[0], m[0], n, false);
+        } else {
+            self.kernel.solve(x, m, n);
+        }
+        let s = &self.kernel.mie;
+        self.x = x[last];
+        self.a.clone_from(&s.a);
+        self.b.clone_from(&s.b);
+        self.abs_sum = s.abs_sum;
+        self.derivatives = false;
+        Ok(LayeredSolution { s: self })
+    }
+
+    /// As [`compute`](Self::compute), also computing the derivatives of the coefficients with
+    /// respect to each layer's size parameter and refractive index: forward-mode dual numbers
+    /// through the kernel, in one pass with 3L derivatives for L ≤ 4 layers and one pass of 3
+    /// per layer above (for one layer, the sphere's closed forms). The values are bitwise those
+    /// of [`compute`](Self::compute).
+    ///
+    /// # Errors
+    ///
+    /// As [`compute`](Self::compute).
+    pub fn compute_with_derivatives(
+        &mut self,
+        x: &[T],
+        m: &[Complex<T>],
+        opts: &MieOptions,
+    ) -> Result<LayeredSolution<'_, T>> {
+        let n = check_inputs(x, m, opts)?;
+        let layers = x.len();
+        self.x = x[layers - 1];
+        self.layers.resize_with(layers, LayerDerivatives::new);
+        self.layers.truncate(layers);
+        match layers {
+            1 => {
+                let s = &mut self.kernel.mie;
+                s.solve(x[0], m[0], n, true);
+                self.a.clone_from(&s.a);
+                self.b.clone_from(&s.b);
+                self.abs_sum = s.abs_sum;
+                let d = &mut self.layers[0];
+                d.da_dx.clone_from(&s.da_dx);
+                d.da_dm.clone_from(&s.da_dm);
+                d.db_dx.clone_from(&s.db_dx);
+                d.db_dm.clone_from(&s.db_dm);
+                d.abs_sum = s.abs_sum_jacobian;
+            }
+            2 => self.dual::<6>(x, m, n, 0),
+            3 => self.dual::<9>(x, m, n, 0),
+            4 => self.dual::<12>(x, m, n, 0),
+            _ => {
+                for l in 0..layers {
+                    self.dual::<3>(x, m, n, l);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        self.derivatives = true;
+        Ok(LayeredSolution { s: self })
+    }
+
+    /// One pass in `Dual<T, N>`, seeding x, Re m and Im m of the N/3 layers from `first`; the
+    /// first pass also stores the values.
+    fn dual<const N: usize>(&mut self, x: &[T], m: &[Complex<T>], n: usize, first: usize)
+    where
+        DualKernels<T>: DualSlot<T, N>,
+    {
+        let seeded = first..first + N / 3;
+        let seed = |l: usize, k: usize, v: T| {
+            let mut eps = [T::zero(); N];
+            if seeded.contains(&l) {
+                eps[3 * (l - first) + k] = T::one();
+            }
+            Dual::new(v, eps)
+        };
+        let xd: Vec<Dual<T, N>> = x.iter().enumerate().map(|(l, &v)| seed(l, 0, v)).collect();
+        let md: Vec<Complex<Dual<T, N>>> = m
+            .iter()
+            .enumerate()
+            .map(|(l, v)| Complex::new(seed(l, 1, v.re), seed(l, 2, v.im)))
+            .collect();
+        let kernel = DualSlot::<T, N>::kernel(&mut self.duals);
+        kernel.solve(&xd, &md, n);
+        let s = &kernel.mie;
+        let value = |z: &Complex<Dual<T, N>>| Complex::new(z.re.re(), z.im.re());
+        if first == 0 {
+            self.a.clear();
+            self.a.extend(s.a.iter().map(value));
+            self.b.clear();
+            self.b.extend(s.b.iter().map(value));
+            self.abs_sum = s.abs_sum.re();
+        }
+        let part = |z: &Complex<Dual<T, N>>, k: usize| Complex::new(z.re.eps()[k], z.im.eps()[k]);
+        for l in seeded.filter(|&l| l < x.len()) {
+            let j = 3 * (l - first);
+            let d = &mut self.layers[l];
+            for (out, c, k) in [
+                (&mut d.da_dx, &s.a, j),
+                (&mut d.da_dm, &s.a, j + 1),
+                (&mut d.db_dx, &s.b, j),
+                (&mut d.db_dm, &s.b, j + 1),
+            ] {
+                out.clear();
+                out.extend(c.iter().map(|z| part(z, k)));
+            }
+            let e = s.abs_sum.eps();
+            d.abs_sum = [e[j], e[j + 1], e[j + 2]];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/// Validates the inputs and returns the number of terms.
+fn check_inputs<T: Real>(x: &[T], m: &[Complex<T>], opts: &MieOptions) -> Result<usize> {
+    let invalid = |s: String| Err(Error::InvalidInput(s));
+    let layers = x.len();
+    if layers == 0 || m.len() != layers {
+        return invalid(format!(
+            "a layered sphere needs one size parameter and one refractive index per layer, got \
+             {} and {}",
+            layers,
+            m.len()
+        ));
+    }
+    if layers > MAX_LAYERS {
+        return invalid(format!(
+            "at most {MAX_LAYERS} layers are accepted, got {layers}"
+        ));
+    }
+    let xs: Vec<f64> = x.iter().map(|v| v.approx_f64()).collect();
+    let ms: Vec<Complex<f64>> = m
+        .iter()
+        .map(|v| Complex::new(v.re.approx_f64(), v.im.approx_f64()))
+        .collect();
+    if let Some((l, v)) = xs.iter().enumerate().find(|(_, v)| !v.is_finite()) {
+        return invalid(format!(
+            "the size parameter of layer {l} must be finite, got {v:e}"
+        ));
+    }
+    if let Some(l) = (1..layers).find(|&l| xs[l] < xs[l - 1]) {
+        return invalid(format!(
+            "the size parameters must be nondecreasing from the core out, but layer {l} has \
+             {:e} < {:e}",
+            xs[l],
+            xs[l - 1]
+        ));
+    }
+    let outer = xs[layers - 1];
+    if xs[0] < MIN_SIZE_PARAMETER || outer > MAX_SIZE_PARAMETER {
+        return invalid(format!(
+            "the size parameters must be in [{MIN_SIZE_PARAMETER:e}, {MAX_SIZE_PARAMETER:e}], \
+             got {:e} to {outer:e}",
+            xs[0]
+        ));
+    }
+    for (l, v) in ms.iter().enumerate() {
+        if !(v.re.is_finite() && v.im.is_finite()) {
+            return invalid(format!(
+                "the refractive index of layer {l} must be finite, got {:e} + {:e}i",
+                v.re, v.im
+            ));
+        }
+        if v.re < 0.0 || v.im < 0.0 {
+            return invalid(format!(
+                "the refractive index of layer {l} must have Re m >= 0 and Im m >= 0 (Im m < 0 \
+                 is a gain medium), got {:e} + {:e}i",
+                v.re, v.im
+            ));
+        }
+        if !(MIN_REFRACTIVE_INDEX..=MAX_REFRACTIVE_INDEX).contains(&v.norm()) {
+            return invalid(format!(
+                "|m| of layer {l} must be in [{MIN_REFRACTIVE_INDEX:e}, \
+                 {MAX_REFRACTIVE_INDEX:e}], got {:e}",
+                v.norm()
+            ));
+        }
+    }
+    let default = default_terms::<T>(outer);
+    let n = opts.n_max.unwrap_or(default);
+    if n == 0 || n > MAX_MIE_ORDER {
+        return invalid(format!(
+            "the number of terms must be in [1, {MAX_MIE_ORDER}], got {n}"
+        ));
+    }
+    if !opts.allow_unvalidated {
+        if !VALIDATED_DOMAIN.contains(&xs, &ms) {
+            return Err(Error::OutsideValidatedDomain(format!(
+                "a layered sphere of {layers} layers with x_L = {outer:e} and m = {ms:?} is \
+                 outside the validated domain {VALIDATED_DOMAIN}"
+            )));
+        }
+        if n < default {
+            return Err(Error::OutsideValidatedDomain(format!(
+                "n_max = {n} is below the default truncation {default} for x_L = {outer:e}"
+            )));
+        }
+    }
+    // The values are checked above; for `Dual` this also rejects non-finite derivative parts.
+    if !(x.iter().all(|v| v.is_finite()) && m.iter().all(|v| v.re.is_finite() && v.im.is_finite()))
+    {
+        return invalid("the derivative parts of x and m must be finite".into());
+    }
+    Ok(n)
+}
+
+/// The solution for one layered sphere: a view of the workspace that computed it.
+#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
+pub struct LayeredSolution<'a, T: Real> {
+    s: &'a Layered<T>,
+}
+
+/// The error for derivatives of a solution computed without them.
+fn no_derivatives(what: &str) -> Error {
+    Error::InvalidInput(format!(
+        "{what} derivatives need a solution from Layered::compute_with_derivatives"
+    ))
+}
+
+impl<'a, T: Real> LayeredSolution<'a, T> {
+    /// The size parameter of the outer surface, x_L.
+    pub fn x(&self) -> T {
+        self.s.x
+    }
+
+    /// The number of terms N.
+    pub fn n_terms(&self) -> usize {
+        self.s.a.len()
+    }
+
+    /// aₙ for n = 1, …, N (index n − 1).
+    pub fn a(&self) -> &'a [Complex<T>] {
+        &self.s.a
+    }
+
+    /// bₙ for n = 1, …, N (index n − 1).
+    pub fn b(&self) -> &'a [Complex<T>] {
+        &self.s.b
+    }
+
+    /// The number of layers whose derivatives were computed (0 without derivatives).
+    fn layers(&self) -> usize {
+        if self.s.derivatives {
+            self.s.layers.len()
+        } else {
+            0
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// The derivatives of the coefficients with respect to the size parameter x_l and the
+    /// refractive index m_l of `layer` (the complex derivative: ∂/∂(Re m_l) = d/dm_l and
+    /// ∂/∂(Im m_l) = i·d/dm_l), if computed by
+    /// [`Layered::compute_with_derivatives`] and `layer` exists.
+    pub fn derivatives(&self, layer: usize) -> Option<CoefficientDerivatives<'a, T>> {
+        (layer < self.layers()).then(|| {
+            let d = &self.s.layers[layer];
+            CoefficientDerivatives {
+                da_dx: &d.da_dx,
+                da_dm: &d.da_dm,
+                db_dx: &d.db_dx,
+                db_dm: &d.db_dm,
+            }
+        })
+    }
+
+    pub(crate) fn coefficients(&self) -> Coefficients<'a, T> {
+        Coefficients {
+            x: self.s.x,
+            a: &self.s.a,
+            b: &self.s.b,
+            abs_sum: self.s.abs_sum,
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// The derivative group of `layer`, if computed.
+    pub(crate) fn group(&self, layer: usize) -> Option<Group<'a, T>> {
+        self.derivatives(layer).map(|d| Group {
+            d,
+            abs_sum: self.s.layers[layer].abs_sum,
+            outer: layer + 1 == self.s.layers.len(),
+        })
+    }
+
+    fn group_or(&self, layer: usize, what: &str) -> Result<Group<'a, T>> {
+        if !self.s.derivatives {
+            return Err(no_derivatives(what));
+        }
+        self.group(layer).ok_or_else(|| {
+            Error::InvalidInput(format!(
+                "no layer {layer}: the particle has {} layers",
+                self.s.layers.len()
+            ))
+        })
+    }
+
+    /// The efficiencies and the asymmetry parameter, per unit geometric cross section of the
+    /// outer surface.
+    pub fn efficiencies(&self) -> Efficiencies<T> {
+        self.coefficients().efficiencies()
+    }
+
+    /// The derivatives of the efficiencies with respect to each layer's x_l, Re m_l and Im m_l
+    /// (one [`EfficiencyJacobian`] per layer), if computed.
+    pub fn efficiencies_jacobian(&self) -> Option<Vec<EfficiencyJacobian<T>>> {
+        if !self.s.derivatives {
+            return None;
+        }
+        let c = self.coefficients();
+        Some(
+            (0..self.layers())
+                .map(|l| c.efficiencies_jacobian(&self.group(l).expect("a layer")))
+                .collect(),
+        )
+    }
+
+    /// S₁ and S₂ at the cosines `mu`, as [`MieSolution::amplitudes`](crate::mie::MieSolution::amplitudes).
+    ///
+    /// # Errors
+    ///
+    /// As [`MieSolution::amplitudes`](crate::mie::MieSolution::amplitudes).
+    pub fn amplitudes(&self, mu: &[T], out: AmplitudeBuffers<'_, T>) -> Result<()> {
+        self.coefficients().amplitudes(mu, out)
+    }
+
+    /// S₁ and S₂ at the cosines of `table`, bitwise as [`amplitudes`](Self::amplitudes).
+    ///
+    /// # Errors
+    ///
+    /// As [`MieSolution::amplitudes_from_table`](crate::mie::MieSolution::amplitudes_from_table).
+    pub fn amplitudes_from_table(
+        &self,
+        table: &AngularTable<T>,
+        out: AmplitudeBuffers<'_, T>,
+    ) -> Result<()> {
+        self.coefficients().amplitudes_from_table(table, out)
+    }
+
+    /// S₁, S₂ and their derivatives with respect to `layer`'s x_l (`d_dx`) and m_l (`d_dm`,
+    /// the complex derivative).
+    ///
+    /// # Errors
+    ///
+    /// As [`amplitudes`](Self::amplitudes), and [`Error::InvalidInput`] without derivatives or
+    /// for a layer that does not exist.
+    pub fn amplitudes_with_derivatives(
+        &self,
+        mu: &[T],
+        layer: usize,
+        values: AmplitudeBuffers<'_, T>,
+        d_dx: AmplitudeBuffers<'_, T>,
+        d_dm: AmplitudeBuffers<'_, T>,
+    ) -> Result<()> {
+        let g = self.group_or(layer, "amplitude")?;
+        self.coefficients()
+            .amplitudes_with_derivatives(mu, &g, values, d_dx, d_dm)
+    }
+
+    /// As [`amplitudes_with_derivatives`](Self::amplitudes_with_derivatives) at the cosines of
+    /// `table` (bitwise).
+    ///
+    /// # Errors
+    ///
+    /// As [`amplitudes_from_table`](Self::amplitudes_from_table), and [`Error::InvalidInput`]
+    /// without derivatives or for a layer that does not exist.
+    pub fn amplitudes_with_derivatives_from_table(
+        &self,
+        table: &AngularTable<T>,
+        layer: usize,
+        values: AmplitudeBuffers<'_, T>,
+        d_dx: AmplitudeBuffers<'_, T>,
+        d_dm: AmplitudeBuffers<'_, T>,
+    ) -> Result<()> {
+        let g = self.group_or(layer, "amplitude")?;
+        self.coefficients()
+            .amplitudes_with_derivatives_from_table(table, &g, values, d_dx, d_dm)
+    }
+
+    /// The phase matrix at the cosines `mu`, as
+    /// [`MieSolution::phase_matrix`](crate::mie::MieSolution::phase_matrix).
+    ///
+    /// # Errors
+    ///
+    /// As [`MieSolution::phase_matrix`](crate::mie::MieSolution::phase_matrix).
+    pub fn phase_matrix(
+        &self,
+        mu: &[T],
+        normalization: Normalization,
+        out: PhaseBuffers<'_, T>,
+    ) -> Result<()> {
+        self.coefficients().phase_matrix(mu, normalization, out)
+    }
+
+    /// The phase matrix at the cosines of `table`, bitwise as
+    /// [`phase_matrix`](Self::phase_matrix).
+    ///
+    /// # Errors
+    ///
+    /// As [`amplitudes_from_table`](Self::amplitudes_from_table).
+    pub fn phase_matrix_from_table(
+        &self,
+        table: &AngularTable<T>,
+        normalization: Normalization,
+        out: PhaseBuffers<'_, T>,
+    ) -> Result<()> {
+        self.coefficients()
+            .phase_matrix_from_table(table, normalization, out)
+    }
+
+    /// The phase matrix and its derivatives with respect to `layer`'s x_l, Re m_l and Im m_l, in
+    /// `d[0]`, `d[1]` and `d[2]`.
+    ///
+    /// # Errors
+    ///
+    /// As [`phase_matrix`](Self::phase_matrix), and [`Error::InvalidInput`] without derivatives
+    /// or for a layer that does not exist.
+    pub fn phase_matrix_with_derivatives(
+        &self,
+        mu: &[T],
+        layer: usize,
+        normalization: Normalization,
+        values: PhaseBuffers<'_, T>,
+        d: [PhaseBuffers<'_, T>; 3],
+    ) -> Result<()> {
+        let g = self.group_or(layer, "phase-matrix")?;
+        self.coefficients()
+            .phase_matrix_with_derivatives(mu, &g, normalization, values, d)
+    }
+
+    /// As [`phase_matrix_with_derivatives`](Self::phase_matrix_with_derivatives) at the cosines
+    /// of `table` (bitwise).
+    ///
+    /// # Errors
+    ///
+    /// As [`phase_matrix_from_table`](Self::phase_matrix_from_table), and
+    /// [`Error::InvalidInput`] without derivatives or for a layer that does not exist.
+    pub fn phase_matrix_with_derivatives_from_table(
+        &self,
+        table: &AngularTable<T>,
+        layer: usize,
+        normalization: Normalization,
+        values: PhaseBuffers<'_, T>,
+        d: [PhaseBuffers<'_, T>; 3],
+    ) -> Result<()> {
+        let g = self.group_or(layer, "phase-matrix")?;
+        self.coefficients()
+            .phase_matrix_with_derivatives_from_table(table, &g, normalization, values, d)
+    }
+
+    /// The Greek coefficients, as [`MieSolution::greek`](crate::mie::MieSolution::greek).
+    ///
+    /// # Errors
+    ///
+    /// As [`MieSolution::greek`](crate::mie::MieSolution::greek).
+    pub fn greek(
+        &self,
+        order: Option<usize>,
+        normalization: Normalization,
+    ) -> Result<GreekCoefficients<T>> {
+        self.coefficients().greek(order, normalization)
+    }
+
+    /// The Greek coefficients and their derivatives with respect to `layer`'s x_l, Re m_l and
+    /// Im m_l.
+    ///
+    /// # Errors
+    ///
+    /// As [`greek`](Self::greek), and [`Error::InvalidInput`] without derivatives or for a
+    /// layer that does not exist.
+    pub fn greek_with_derivatives(
+        &self,
+        order: Option<usize>,
+        layer: usize,
+        normalization: Normalization,
+    ) -> Result<(GreekCoefficients<T>, [GreekCoefficients<T>; 3])> {
+        let g = self.group_or(layer, "Greek")?;
+        self.coefficients()
+            .greek_with_derivatives(order, &g, normalization)
+    }
+}

 //! ψ_k(ρ)/ψ_{k−1}(ρ), D_n(ρ) = (n + 1)/ρ − u_{n+1}(ρ), R_n = ξ_{n−1}/ξ_n, T_n = ψ_n/ξ_n, and

+//!
+//! A layered sphere has the same outer step with D_n(mx) replaced by the effective
+//! log-derivatives Hₐ and H_b at its outer surface (`Outer`), which `crate::layered` computes.
 
 use num_complex::Complex;
 
 use crate::complex::inv;
 use crate::scalar::Real;
 
+/// One coefficient's effective log-derivative H at the outer surface of a layered sphere, at
+/// one order: as the correction δ = H − D_n(mx) to the outer layer's own log-derivative, which
+/// keeps the ratio region's numerators free of cancellation, or as H itself, near the poles of
+/// D_n(mx) where D_n(mx) and δ would cancel.
+#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
+pub(crate) enum Side<T> {
+    Delta(Complex<T>),
+    Direct(Complex<T>),
+}
+
+/// Hₐ and H_b at one order.
+pub(crate) type Outer<T> = (Side<T>, Side<T>);
+

+/// u_k(z) for k = 1, …, n_top + 1 into `out` (index k), complex z.
+pub(crate) fn ratios_complex<T: Real>(out: &mut Vec<Complex<T>>, z: Complex<T>, n_top: usize) {
+    let inv_z = inv(z);
+    let start = start_index::<T>(n_top + 1, z.norm().approx_f64());
+    out.clear();
+    out.resize(n_top + 2, real(T::zero()));
+    let mut u = real(T::zero());
+    for k in (1..=start).rev() {
+        let c = inv_z.scale(int(2 * k + 1));
+        let mut den = c - u;
+        if den.re == T::zero() && den.im == T::zero() {
+            // Only at an exact zero of ψ_{k−1}: nudge by a rounding error, as Lentz's
+            // method does, so that the pole of D_{k−1} stays finite.
+            den = real(T::epsilon() * (c.norm() + u.norm()));
+        }
+        u = inv(den);
+        if k <= n_top + 1 {
+            out[k] = u;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/// u_k(ρ) for k = from, …, n_top + 1 (real ρ), returned at index k of a vector.
+pub(crate) fn ratios_real<T: Real>(out: &mut Vec<T>, rho: T, from: usize, n_top: usize) {
+    let inv_rho = rho.recip();
+    let start = start_index::<T>(n_top + 1, rho.approx_f64().abs());
+    out.clear();
+    out.resize(n_top + 2, T::zero());
+    let mut u = T::zero();
+    for k in (from.max(1)..=start).rev() {
+        let c = inv_rho * int::<T>(2 * k + 1);
+        let mut den = c - u;
+        if den == T::zero() {
+            den = T::epsilon() * (c.abs() + u.abs());
+        }
+        u = den.recip();
+        if k <= n_top + 1 {
+            out[k] = u;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/// u_k(z) for z = m·x and k = 1, …, n_top + 1 into `out` (index k): by the real recurrence
+/// (through `scratch`) when Im m is zero (with no derivative seeded in it), as for D_n(mx).
+pub(crate) fn ratios<T: Real>(
+    out: &mut Vec<Complex<T>>,
+    scratch: &mut Vec<T>,
+    m: Complex<T>,
+    x: T,
+    n_top: usize,
+) {
+    let z = m.scale(x);
+    if m.im.is_zero() {
+        ratios_real(scratch, z.re, 1, n_top);
+        out.clear();
+        out.extend(scratch.iter().map(|&u| real(u)));
+    } else {
+        ratios_complex(out, z, n_top);
+    }
+}
