diff --git a/scatrs/tests/layered_accuracy_map.rs b/scatrs/tests/layered_accuracy_map.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..10a5e18
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scatrs/tests/layered_accuracy_map.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,392 @@
+//! The accuracy map of the layered-sphere outputs: `f64` against `DoubleDouble` on a dense grid
+//! over the validated domain, every output within its declared tolerance.
+//!
+//! The grid: the outer size parameters of the sphere map (x_L = d·10^e, up to the domain's 10⁴,
+//! 101 values) against `PARTICLES`: two-layer particles of the pairs of indices `PAIRS` with the
+//! core fractions `FRACTIONS`, and three- and ten-layer particles. The amplitudes are at the
+//! sphere map's cosines. `DoubleDouble` is within 16 times the declared constants, in units of
+//! (κ′ + cond)·2⁻¹⁰⁴, of Arb on every output (tests/layered_reference.rs), so it stands in for
+//! the exact values. The derivative columns cover the derivatives' domain (x_L ≤ 100) for the
+//! particles of at most four layers (the ten-layer particle takes other passes of the same code,
+//! which tests/layered.rs checks against differences); above it they are null.
+//!
+//! Each error is reported as C = error/((κ′ + cond)·2⁻⁵²); the test prints the worst C of each
+//! output. With `SCATRS_ACCURACY_MAP=path` it also writes every C as JSON, which the
+//! documentation plots (docs/validation/accuracy.md):
+//!
+//! ```text
+//! SCATRS_ACCURACY_MAP=$PWD/docs/validation/data/layered-accuracy-map.json \
+//!     cargo test -p scatrs --release --test layered_accuracy_map -- --nocapture
+//! ```
+#![allow(clippy::needless_range_loop)] // parallel per-layer arrays are indexed together
+
+mod common;
+
+use std::fmt::Write as _;
+use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
+
+use common::layered::{
+    Measure, Outputs, derivative_measures, q_of, reference, solve, value_measures,
+};
+use num_complex::Complex;
+use scatrs::layered::{VALIDATED_DOMAIN, ValidatedDomain};
+use scatrs::mie::MieOutput;
+use scatrs::scalar::DoubleDouble as Dd;
+
+const MANTISSAS: [&str; 10] = ["1", "1.25", "1.6", "2", "2.5", "3.2", "4", "5", "6.4", "8"];
+
+/// (core, shell) as (Re m, Im m): black carbon in sulfate, water on ice, a silica core in a
+/// gold-like shell, absorbing cores in a clear shell, high contrast both ways, near unity, an air
+/// shell, a clear core in an absorbing shell, a metallic core, a high-index core.
+const PAIRS: [((f64, f64), (f64, f64)); 12] = [
+    ((1.95, 0.79), (1.43, 1e-8)),
+    ((1.31, 1e-8), (1.33, 1e-9)),
+    ((1.45, 0.0), (0.2, 3.0)),
+    ((1.5, 1e-8), (1.33, 0.0)),
+    ((1.5, 1e-3), (1.33, 0.0)),
+    ((1.5, 1.0), (1.33, 0.0)),
+    ((10.0, 10.0), (1.33, 0.0)),
+    ((1.33, 0.0), (10.0, 0.0)),
+    ((1.01, 0.0), (1.0001, 0.0)),
+    ((1.5, 0.01), (1.0, 0.0)),
+    ((1.33, 0.0), (1.5, 0.1)),
+    ((0.2, 3.0), (1.5, 0.0)),
+];
+
+/// Core radius / outer radius.
+const FRACTIONS: [f64; 4] = [1e-3, 0.3, 0.9, 0.999];
+
+/// A particle of the grid: the radius fractions of its interfaces (the last 1) and its indices.
+struct Particle {
+    fractions: Vec<f64>,
+    m: Vec<Complex<f64>>,
+}
+
+fn particles() -> Vec<Particle> {
+    let c = |(r, i): (f64, f64)| Complex::new(r, i);
+    let mut out: Vec<Particle> = PAIRS
+        .iter()
+        .flat_map(|&(core, shell)| {
+            FRACTIONS.iter().map(move |&f| Particle {
+                fractions: vec![f, 1.0],
+                m: vec![c(core), c(shell)],
+            })
+        })
+        .collect();
+    out.push(Particle {
+        fractions: vec![0.3, 0.7, 1.0],
+        m: vec![c((1.5, 0.1)), c((2.5, 1.0)), c((1.33, 0.0))],
+    });
+    out.push(Particle {
+        fractions: vec![0.5, 0.8, 1.0],
+        m: vec![c((1.45, 0.0)), c((0.2, 3.0)), c((1.33, 0.0))],
+    });
+    out.push(Particle {
+        fractions: (1..=10).map(|l| f64::from(l) / 10.0).collect(),
+        m: (1..=10)
+            .map(|l| c((1.6 - 0.02 * f64::from(l), 1e-4)))
+            .collect(),
+    });
+    out
+}
+
+/// The columns of the map.
+const OUTPUTS: [(&str, MieOutput); 10] = [
+    ("coefficients", MieOutput::Coefficients),
+    ("q_ext", MieOutput::Extinction),
+    ("q_sca", MieOutput::Scattering),
+    ("q_abs", MieOutput::Absorption),
+    ("q_back", MieOutput::Backscattering),
+    ("g", MieOutput::Asymmetry),
+    ("amplitudes", MieOutput::Amplitudes),
+    ("coefficient_derivatives", MieOutput::CoefficientDerivatives),
+    ("efficiency_jacobian", MieOutput::EfficiencyJacobian),
+    ("amplitude_derivatives", MieOutput::AmplitudeDerivatives),
+];
+
+fn size_parameters() -> Vec<f64> {
+    (-6..=4)
+        .flat_map(|e| MANTISSAS.iter().map(move |d| format!("{d}e{e}")))
+        .map(|s| s.parse::<f64>().expect("a decimal literal"))
+        .filter(|&x| x <= VALIDATED_DOMAIN.x_max)
+        .collect()
+}
+
+/// As the sphere map's: −1 + k/16 and 1 − 2^-2j.
+fn cosines() -> Vec<f64> {
+    let uniform = (0..=32).map(|k| -1.0 + f64::from(k) / 16.0);
+    let forward = (3..=10).map(|j| 1.0 - 2f64.powi(-2 * j));
+    uniform.chain(forward).collect()
+}
+
+fn diff(a: f64, b: Dd) -> f64 {
+    (Dd::from_f64(a) - b).hi().abs()
+}
+
+fn cdiff(a: Complex<f64>, b: Complex<Dd>) -> f64 {
+    diff(a.re, b.re).hypot(diff(a.im, b.im))
+}
+
+fn array_error(f: &[Complex<f64>], d: &[Complex<Dd>]) -> f64 {
+    f.iter()
+        .zip(d)
+        .map(|(&a, &b)| cdiff(a, b))
+        .fold(0.0, f64::max)
+}
+
+/// C of every output of the particle `p` at outer size parameter `x_l`, ordered as `OUTPUTS`.
+fn point(x_l: f64, p: &Particle, mu: &[f64]) -> [f64; 10] {
+    let x: Vec<f64> = p.fractions.iter().map(|f| f * x_l).collect();
+    let m = &p.m;
+    let layers = x.len();
+    let derivatives = layers <= 4 && VALIDATED_DOMAIN.contains_derivatives(&x, m);
+    let f: Outputs<f64> = solve(&x, m, mu, None);
+    let n = f.a.len();
+    let d = reference(&x, m, mu, Some(n));
+    let orders: Vec<usize> = (1..=n).collect();
+    let v = value_measures(&x, m, &d, &orders);
+    let c = |output: MieOutput, err: f64, measure: Measure| {
+        let tol = VALIDATED_DOMAIN.tolerance(output, x_l, m, measure.condition);
+        assert!(
+            measure.scale > 0.0 && tol.is_finite(),
+            "{output:?} at x = {x:?}, m = {m:?}: degenerate measure {measure:?}, tolerance {tol:e}"
+        );
+        err / measure.scale / tol * ValidatedDomain::constant(output)
+    };
+    let mut out = [0.0f64; 10];
+    let mut put = |k: usize, value: f64| {
+        out[k] = out[k].max(if value.is_nan() { f64::INFINITY } else { value });
+    };
+    put(
+        0,
+        c(
+            MieOutput::Coefficients,
+            array_error(&f.a, &d.a),
+            v.coefficients[0],
+        ),
+    );
+    put(
+        0,
+        c(
+            MieOutput::Coefficients,
+            array_error(&f.b, &d.b),
+            v.coefficients[1],
+        ),
+    );
+    let real = m.iter().all(|v| v.im == 0.0);
+    for k in 0..5 {
+        if k == 2 && real {
+            put(3, if f.q.q_abs == 0.0 { 0.0 } else { f64::INFINITY });
+            continue;
+        }
+        let err = diff(q_of(&f.q, k), q_of(&d.q, k));
+        put(1 + k, c(OUTPUTS[1 + k].1, err, v.efficiencies[k]));
+    }
+    for j in 0..mu.len() {
+        for i in 0..2 {
+            let err = cdiff(f.s[i][j], d.s[i][j]);
+            put(6, c(MieOutput::Amplitudes, err, v.amplitudes[j][i]));
+        }
+    }
+    if derivatives {
+        let dm = derivative_measures(&x, m, mu, &d, &orders);
+        for l in 0..layers {
+            for k in 0..4 {
+                let err = array_error(&f.d[l][k], &d.d[l][k]);
+                put(
+                    7,
+                    c(
+                        MieOutput::CoefficientDerivatives,
+                        err,
+                        dm.coefficients[l][k],
+                    ),
+                );
+            }
+            for p in 0..3 {
+                for k in 0..5 {
+                    if k == 2 && real && p < 2 {
+                        let exact = q_of(&f.jac[l][p], k) == 0.0;
+                        put(8, if exact { 0.0 } else { f64::INFINITY });
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    let err = diff(q_of(&f.jac[l][p], k), q_of(&d.jac[l][p], k));
+                    put(
+                        8,
+                        c(MieOutput::EfficiencyJacobian, err, dm.jacobian[l][p][k]),
+                    );
+                }
+            }
+            for p in 0..2 {
+                for i in 0..2 {
+                    let k = 2 * p + i;
+                    let err = (0..mu.len())
+                        .map(|j| cdiff(f.ds[l][k][j], d.ds[l][k][j]))
+                        .fold(0.0, f64::max);
+                    put(
+                        9,
+                        c(MieOutput::AmplitudeDerivatives, err, dm.amplitudes[l][p][i]),
+                    );
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out
+}
+
+fn to_json(xs: &[f64], ps: &[Particle], results: &[[f64; 10]]) -> String {
+    let list = |v: Vec<String>| v.join(", ");
+    let mut json = String::from("{\n");
+    let _ = writeln!(
+        json,
+        "  \"x\": [{}],",
+        list(xs.iter().map(|x| format!("{x:e}")).collect())
+    );
+    let particles = ps
+        .iter()
+        .map(|p| {
+            let f = list(p.fractions.iter().map(|v| format!("{v:e}")).collect());
+            let m = list(
+                p.m.iter()
+                    .map(|v| format!("[{:e}, {:e}]", v.re, v.im))
+                    .collect(),
+            );
+            format!("{{\"fractions\": [{f}], \"m\": [{m}]}}")
+        })
+        .collect();
+    let _ = writeln!(json, "  \"particles\": [{}],", list(particles));
+    let declared = OUTPUTS
+        .iter()
+        .map(|(n, o)| format!("\"{n}\": {}", ValidatedDomain::constant(*o)))
+        .collect();
+    let _ = writeln!(json, "  \"declared\": {{{}}},", list(declared));
+    json += "  \"c\": {\n";
+    for (o, (name, _)) in OUTPUTS.iter().enumerate() {
+        let rows = (0..xs.len())
+            .map(|i| {
+                let row = (0..ps.len())
+                    .map(|j| match results[i * ps.len() + j][o] {
+                        c if c.is_finite() => format!("{c:.3e}"),
+                        _ => "null".to_owned(),
+                    })
+                    .collect();
+                format!("[{}]", list(row))
+            })
+            .collect();
+        let comma = if o + 1 < OUTPUTS.len() { "," } else { "" };
+        let _ = writeln!(json, "    \"{name}\": [{}]{comma}", list(rows));
+    }
+    json + "  }\n}\n"
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn accuracy_map_is_within_the_declared_tolerances() {
+    let xs = size_parameters();
+    assert_eq!(xs.len(), 101, "size parameters");
+    let ps = particles();
+    let mu = cosines();
+    let points: Vec<(f64, usize)> = xs
+        .iter()
+        .flat_map(|&x| (0..ps.len()).map(move |j| (x, j)))
+        .collect();
+    let inside = |&(x, j): &(f64, usize)| {
+        let p: &Particle = &ps[j];
+        let xl: Vec<f64> = p.fractions.iter().map(|f| f * x).collect();
+        VALIDATED_DOMAIN.contains(&xl, &p.m)
+    };
+    let next = AtomicUsize::new(0);
+    let threads = std::thread::available_parallelism().map_or(1, |n| n.get());
+    let done: Vec<Vec<(usize, [f64; 10])>> = std::thread::scope(|scope| {
+        let workers: Vec<_> = (0..threads)
+            .map(|_| {
+                scope.spawn(|| {
+                    let mut mine = Vec::new();
+                    loop {
+                        let k = next.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
+                        let Some(&(x, j)) = points.get(k) else {
+                            break mine;
+                        };
+                        // Outside the domain: no columns (null in the map).
+                        let c = if inside(&(x, j)) {
+                            point(x, &ps[j], &mu)
+                        } else {
+                            [f64::NAN; 10]
+                        };
+                        mine.push((k, c));
+                    }
+                })
+            })
+            .collect();
+        workers
+            .into_iter()
+            .map(|w| w.join().expect("worker"))
+            .collect()
+    });
+    let mut results = vec![[0.0f64; 10]; points.len()];
+    let done: Vec<(usize, [f64; 10])> = done.into_iter().flatten().collect();
+    assert_eq!(done.len(), points.len(), "every point computed");
+    for (k, c) in done {
+        results[k] = c;
+    }
+    if let Ok(path) = std::env::var("SCATRS_ACCURACY_MAP") {
+        std::fs::write(&path, to_json(&xs, &ps, &results)).expect("write the map");
+        eprintln!("wrote {path}");
+    }
+    // With SCATRS_MAP_WORST, the worst points of the derivative columns.
+    if std::env::var("SCATRS_MAP_WORST").is_ok() {
+        for o in 7..10 {
+            let mut v: Vec<(f64, usize)> = results
+                .iter()
+                .enumerate()
+                .filter(|&(k, _)| inside(&points[k]) && points[k].0 <= 100.0)
+                .map(|(k, c)| (c[o], k))
+                .collect();
+            v.sort_by(|a, b| b.0.total_cmp(&a.0));
+            for &(c, k) in v.iter().take(8) {
+                let (x, j) = points[k];
+                eprintln!(
+                    "{} C {c:.0} x_L {x:e} fractions {:?} m {:?}",
+                    OUTPUTS[o].0,
+                    ps[j].fractions,
+                    ps[j].m.iter().map(|m| (m.re, m.im)).collect::<Vec<_>>()
+                );
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    let mut failures = Vec::new();
+    for (o, (name, output)) in OUTPUTS.iter().enumerate() {
+        let (k, worst) = results
+            .iter()
+            .enumerate()
+            .filter(|&(k, _)| inside(&points[k]))
+            .map(|(k, c)| (k, c[o]))
+            .fold(
+                (0, 0.0),
+                |a, b| if b.1.is_nan() || b.1 > a.1 { b } else { a },
+            );
+        // The worst C per decade of x_L, to see where an output degrades.
+        let mut decades = [0.0f64; 11];
+        for (k, c) in results.iter().enumerate() {
+            if inside(&points[k]) {
+                let d = (points[k].0.log10().floor() as i32 + 6).clamp(0, 10) as usize;
+                decades[d] = decades[d].max(c[o]);
+            }
+        }
+        eprintln!(
+            "{name:<24} by decade from 1e-6: {:?}",
+            decades.map(|c| (c * 10.0).round() / 10.0)
+        );
+        let declared = ValidatedDomain::constant(*output);
+        let (x, j) = points[k];
+        eprintln!(
+            "{name:<24} worst C = {worst:8.3} (declared {declared}) at x_L = {x:e}, fractions {:?}, m = {:?}",
+            ps[j].fractions, ps[j].m
+        );
+        if worst.is_nan() || worst > declared {
+            failures.push(*name);
+        }
+    }
+    assert!(
+        failures.is_empty(),
+        "outside the declared tolerance: {failures:?}"
+    );
+}
diff --git a/scatrs/tests/layered_greek_accuracy_map.rs b/scatrs/tests/layered_greek_accuracy_map.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..5b6bb4d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scatrs/tests/layered_greek_accuracy_map.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,443 @@
+//! The accuracy map of the phase matrix and the Greek coefficients of layered spheres: `f64`
+//! against `DoubleDouble` on a grid over the validated domain, every output within its declared
+//! tolerance.
+//!
+//! The grid: x_L = d·10^e for the mantissas `MANTISSAS` and e = −6, …, 3, up to 10³ (46 values),
+//! against `PARTICLES`, with the phase matrix at the cosines of the sphere's Greek map,
+//! normalized, and the Greek coefficients up to all orders (2N) for x_L ≤ 160 and order 100
+//! above, as the sphere's Greek map (`tests/mie_greek_accuracy_map.rs`) has them. The derivative
+//! columns cover the derivatives' domain (x_L ≤ 100). The measures are the sphere's, with the
+//! condition numbers summed over every layer's x_l, Re m_l and Im m_l (`tests/common/layered.rs`).
+//!
+//! With `SCATRS_ACCURACY_MAP=path` the test writes every C as JSON:
+//!
+//! ```text
+//! SCATRS_ACCURACY_MAP=$PWD/docs/validation/data/layered-greek-accuracy-map.json \
+//!     cargo test -p scatrs --release --test layered_greek_accuracy_map -- --nocapture
+//! ```
+#![allow(clippy::needless_range_loop)] // parallel per-layer arrays are indexed together
+
+mod common;
+
+use std::fmt::Write as _;
+use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
+
+use common::check::max_nan;
+use common::greek::{buffers, greek_arrays};
+use common::mie::Measure;
+use num_complex::Complex;
+use scatrs::Real;
+use scatrs::layered::{Layered, VALIDATED_DOMAIN, ValidatedDomain};
+use scatrs::mie::{MieOptions, MieOutput, Normalization};
+use scatrs::scalar::DoubleDouble as Dd;
+
+const MANTISSAS: [&str; 5] = ["1", "1.6", "2.5", "4", "6.4"];
+const X_MAX: f64 = 1e3;
+const ALL_ORDERS_UP_TO: f64 = 160.0;
+const ORDER_ABOVE: usize = 100;
+
+/// Two-layer particles (core fractions 0.3 and 0.9) of the pairs of the layered accuracy map,
+/// and a three-layer particle.
+fn particles() -> Vec<(Vec<f64>, Vec<Complex<f64>>)> {
+    let c = |r: f64, i: f64| Complex::new(r, i);
+    let pairs = [
+        (c(1.95, 0.79), c(1.43, 1e-8)),
+        (c(1.31, 1e-8), c(1.33, 1e-9)),
+        (c(1.45, 0.0), c(0.2, 3.0)),
+        (c(1.5, 1e-8), c(1.33, 0.0)),
+        (c(1.5, 1.0), c(1.33, 0.0)),
+        (c(10.0, 10.0), c(1.33, 0.0)),
+        (c(1.33, 0.0), c(10.0, 0.0)),
+        (c(1.01, 0.0), c(1.0001, 0.0)),
+        (c(1.5, 0.01), c(1.0, 0.0)),
+        (c(1.33, 0.0), c(1.5, 0.1)),
+    ];
+    let mut out: Vec<_> = pairs
+        .iter()
+        .flat_map(|&(a, b)| [0.3, 0.9].map(|f| (vec![f, 1.0], vec![a, b])))
+        .collect();
+    out.push((
+        vec![0.3, 0.7, 1.0],
+        vec![c(1.5, 0.1), c(2.5, 1.0), c(1.33, 0.0)],
+    ));
+    out
+}
+
+const OUTPUTS: [&str; 4] = [
+    "phase_matrix",
+    "greek",
+    "phase_matrix_derivatives",
+    "greek_derivatives",
+];
+
+fn size_parameters() -> Vec<f64> {
+    (-6..=3)
+        .flat_map(|e| MANTISSAS.iter().map(move |d| format!("{d}e{e}")))
+        .map(|s| s.parse::<f64>().expect("a decimal literal"))
+        .filter(|&x| x <= X_MAX)
+        .collect()
+}
+
+/// As the sphere's Greek map: −1 + k/8 and 1 − 2^-2j.
+fn cosines() -> Vec<f64> {
+    let uniform = (0..=16).map(|k| -1.0 + f64::from(k) / 8.0);
+    let forward = (3..=8).map(|j| 1.0 - 2f64.powi(-2 * j));
+    uniform.chain(forward).collect()
+}
+
+/// The phase matrix and Greek coefficients, with their derivatives per layer.
+#[derive(Clone)]
+struct Out<T> {
+    greek: [Vec<T>; 6],
+    /// Per layer: ∂/∂x_l, ∂/∂Re m_l, ∂/∂Im m_l.
+    greek_d: Vec<[[Vec<T>; 6]; 3]>,
+    phase: [Vec<T>; 4],
+    phase_d: Vec<[[Vec<T>; 4]; 3]>,
+}
+
+fn solve<T: Real>(
+    x: &[T],
+    m: &[Complex<T>],
+    mu: &[T],
+    n: Option<usize>,
+    order: usize,
+    derivatives: bool,
+) -> Out<T> {
+    let mut opts = MieOptions::new().with_allow_unvalidated(true);
+    if let Some(n) = n {
+        opts = opts.with_n_max(n);
+    }
+    let mut lay = Layered::<T>::new();
+    let norm = Normalization::Normalized;
+    let zeros = || vec![T::zero(); mu.len()];
+    let sol = if derivatives {
+        lay.compute_with_derivatives(x, m, &opts)
+    } else {
+        lay.compute(x, m, &opts)
+    }
+    .expect("valid inputs");
+    let greek = greek_arrays(&sol.greek(Some(order), norm).expect("valid order"));
+    let mut phase: [Vec<T>; 4] = std::array::from_fn(|_| zeros());
+    sol.phase_matrix(mu, norm, buffers(&mut phase))
+        .expect("valid cosines");
+    let (mut greek_d, mut phase_d) = (Vec::new(), Vec::new());
+    if derivatives {
+        for l in 0..x.len() {
+            let (_, dg) = sol
+                .greek_with_derivatives(Some(order), l, norm)
+                .expect("valid order");
+            greek_d.push(dg.each_ref().map(greek_arrays));
+            let mut v: [Vec<T>; 4] = std::array::from_fn(|_| zeros());
+            let mut d: [[Vec<T>; 4]; 3] = std::array::from_fn(|_| std::array::from_fn(|_| zeros()));
+            {
+                let [dx, dr, di] = &mut d;
+                sol.phase_matrix_with_derivatives(
+                    mu,
+                    l,
+                    norm,
+                    buffers(&mut v),
+                    [buffers(dx), buffers(dr), buffers(di)],
+                )
+                .expect("valid cosines");
+            }
+            phase_d.push(d);
+        }
+    }
+    Out {
+        greek,
+        greek_d,
+        phase,
+        phase_d,
+    }
+}
+
+fn dd_inputs(x: &[f64], m: &[Complex<f64>]) -> (Vec<Dd>, Vec<Complex<Dd>>) {
+    (
+        x.iter().map(|&v| Dd::from_f64(v)).collect(),
+        m.iter()
+            .map(|v| Complex::new(Dd::from_f64(v.re), Dd::from_f64(v.im)))
+            .collect(),
+    )
+}
+
+fn diff(a: f64, b: Dd) -> f64 {
+    (Dd::from_f64(a) - b).hi().abs()
+}
+
+/// Σ_l x_l|∂y/∂x_l| + |Re m_l||∂y/∂(Re m_l)| + |Im m_l||∂y/∂(Im m_l)|.
+fn sensitivity(x: &[f64], m: &[Complex<f64>], d: &dyn Fn(usize, usize) -> f64) -> f64 {
+    (0..x.len())
+        .map(|l| {
+            x[l] * d(l, 0).abs() + m[l].re.abs() * d(l, 1).abs() + m[l].im.abs() * d(l, 2).abs()
+        })
+        .sum()
+}
+
+/// C of every output of the particle (`fractions`, `m`) at x_L, ordered as `OUTPUTS` (NaN for
+/// the derivative columns outside the derivatives' domain).
+fn point(x_l: f64, fractions: &[f64], m: &[Complex<f64>], mu: &[f64]) -> [f64; 4] {
+    let x: Vec<f64> = fractions.iter().map(|f| f * x_l).collect();
+    let layers = x.len();
+    let order = if x_l <= ALL_ORDERS_UP_TO {
+        2 * scatrs::mie::default_n_max(x_l)
+    } else {
+        ORDER_ABOVE
+    };
+    let derivatives = VALIDATED_DOMAIN.contains_derivatives(&x, m);
+    let f = solve(&x, m, mu, None, order, derivatives);
+    let n = scatrs::mie::default_n_max(x_l);
+    let (xd, md) = dd_inputs(&x, m);
+    let mu_dd: Vec<Dd> = mu.iter().map(|&v| Dd::from_f64(v)).collect();
+    let d = solve(&xd, &md, &mu_dd, Some(n), order, true);
+    let tol = |output: MieOutput, measure: Measure| {
+        let t = VALIDATED_DOMAIN.tolerance(output, x_l, m, measure.condition);
+        assert!(
+            measure.scale > 0.0 && t.is_finite(),
+            "{output:?} at x = {x:?}, m = {m:?}: degenerate measure {measure:?}"
+        );
+        t
+    };
+    let c = |output: MieOutput, err: f64, measure: Measure| {
+        err / measure.scale / tol(output, measure) * ValidatedDomain::constant(output)
+    };
+    let mut out = [0.0f64; 4];
+    let alpha = d.greek[0].iter().map(|v| v.hi().abs()).fold(0.0, max_nan);
+    let mean = d.greek[0][0].hi();
+    let greek_out = MieOutput::GreekCoefficients { order };
+    for k in 0..6 {
+        let num = (0..d.greek[k].len())
+            .map(|l| sensitivity(&x, m, &|q, p| d.greek_d[q][p][k][l].hi()))
+            .fold(0.0, max_nan);
+        let measure = Measure {
+            scale: alpha,
+            condition: num / alpha,
+        };
+        let err = f.greek[k]
+            .iter()
+            .zip(&d.greek[k])
+            .map(|(&a, &b)| diff(a, b))
+            .fold(0.0, max_nan);
+        out[1] = max_nan(out[1], c(greek_out, err, measure));
+    }
+    for j in 0..mu.len() {
+        let scale = d.phase[0][j].hi().max(mean);
+        for k in 0..4 {
+            let condition = sensitivity(&x, m, &|q, p| d.phase_d[q][p][k][j].hi()) / scale;
+            let err = diff(f.phase[k][j], d.phase[k][j]);
+            out[0] = max_nan(
+                out[0],
+                c(MieOutput::PhaseMatrix, err, Measure { scale, condition }),
+            );
+        }
+    }
+    if !derivatives {
+        out[2] = f64::NAN;
+        out[3] = f64::NAN;
+        return out;
+    }
+    // Condition numbers of the derivatives from central differences of double-double
+    // derivatives, steps 2⁻³⁰ x_l and 2⁻³⁰ |m_l| (one-sided where needed, as
+    // `common::layered::derivative_measures`).
+    let h = 2f64.powi(-30);
+    let big = m.iter().map(|v| v.norm()).fold(1.0, f64::max);
+    let phase_of_x = 1.0 + big * x_l;
+    let mut steps = Vec::new();
+    for l in 0..layers {
+        let mut per = Vec::new();
+        for p in 0..3 {
+            let hp = if p == 0 { x[l] * h } else { m[l].norm() * h };
+            let up = p != 0 || l + 1 == layers || x[l] + hp <= x[l + 1];
+            let down = match p {
+                0 => l == 0 || x[l] - hp >= x[l - 1],
+                1 => true,
+                _ => m[l].im >= hp,
+            };
+            let at = |sign: f64| {
+                let (mut xs, mut ms) = (xd.clone(), md.clone());
+                let s = Dd::from_f64(sign * hp);
+                match p {
+                    0 => xs[l] += s,
+                    1 => ms[l].re += s,
+                    _ => ms[l].im += s,
+                }
+                solve(&xs, &ms, &mu_dd, Some(n), order, true)
+            };
+            per.push(match (up, down) {
+                (true, true) => (at(1.0), at(-1.0), 2.0 * hp),
+                (true, false) => (at(1.0), d.clone(), hp),
+                _ => (d.clone(), at(-1.0), hp),
+            });
+        }
+        steps.push(per);
+    }
+    let fd = |a: Dd, b: Dd, w: f64| ((a - b) / Dd::from_f64(w)).hi();
+    let greek_d_out = MieOutput::GreekDerivatives { order };
+    for l in 0..layers {
+        for p in 0..3 {
+            let p_size = if p == 0 { x[l] } else { m[l].norm() };
+            for k in 0..6 {
+                let natural = alpha * phase_of_x / p_size;
+                let values = &d.greek_d[l][p][k];
+                let scale = values
+                    .iter()
+                    .map(|v| v.hi().abs().max(natural))
+                    .fold(0.0, max_nan);
+                let num = (0..values.len())
+                    .map(|o| {
+                        sensitivity(&x, m, &|q, r| {
+                            let (pl, mi, w) = &steps[q][r];
+                            fd(pl.greek_d[l][p][k][o], mi.greek_d[l][p][k][o], *w)
+                        })
+                    })
+                    .fold(0.0, max_nan);
+                let measure = Measure {
+                    scale,
+                    condition: num / scale,
+                };
+                let err = f.greek_d[l][p][k]
+                    .iter()
+                    .zip(values)
+                    .map(|(&a, &b)| diff(a, b))
+                    .fold(0.0, max_nan);
+                out[3] = max_nan(out[3], c(greek_d_out, err, measure));
+            }
+            for k in 0..4 {
+                let scale = (0..mu.len())
+                    .map(|j| {
+                        let natural = d.phase[0][j].hi().max(mean) * phase_of_x / p_size;
+                        d.phase_d[l][p][k][j].hi().abs().max(natural)
+                    })
+                    .fold(0.0, max_nan);
+                let num = (0..mu.len())
+                    .map(|j| {
+                        sensitivity(&x, m, &|q, r| {
+                            let (pl, mi, w) = &steps[q][r];
+                            fd(pl.phase_d[l][p][k][j], mi.phase_d[l][p][k][j], *w)
+                        })
+                    })
+                    .fold(0.0, max_nan);
+                let measure = Measure {
+                    scale,
+                    condition: num / scale,
+                };
+                let err = (0..mu.len())
+                    .map(|j| diff(f.phase_d[l][p][k][j], d.phase_d[l][p][k][j]))
+                    .fold(0.0, max_nan);
+                out[2] = max_nan(out[2], c(MieOutput::PhaseMatrixDerivatives, err, measure));
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out
+}
+
+fn to_json(xs: &[f64], ps: &[(Vec<f64>, Vec<Complex<f64>>)], results: &[[f64; 4]]) -> String {
+    let list = |v: Vec<String>| v.join(", ");
+    let mut json = String::from("{\n");
+    let _ = writeln!(
+        json,
+        "  \"x\": [{}],",
+        list(xs.iter().map(|x| format!("{x:e}")).collect())
+    );
+    let particles = ps
+        .iter()
+        .map(|(f, m)| {
+            let f = list(f.iter().map(|v| format!("{v:e}")).collect());
+            let m = list(
+                m.iter()
+                    .map(|v| format!("[{:e}, {:e}]", v.re, v.im))
+                    .collect(),
+            );
+            format!("{{\"fractions\": [{f}], \"m\": [{m}]}}")
+        })
+        .collect();
+    let _ = writeln!(json, "  \"particles\": [{}],", list(particles));
+    json += "  \"c\": {\n";
+    for (o, name) in OUTPUTS.iter().enumerate() {
+        let rows = (0..xs.len())
+            .map(|i| {
+                let row = (0..ps.len())
+                    .map(|j| match results[i * ps.len() + j][o] {
+                        c if c.is_finite() => format!("{c:.3e}"),
+                        _ => "null".to_owned(),
+                    })
+                    .collect();
+                format!("[{}]", list(row))
+            })
+            .collect();
+        let comma = if o + 1 < OUTPUTS.len() { "," } else { "" };
+        let _ = writeln!(json, "    \"{name}\": [{}]{comma}", list(rows));
+    }
+    json + "  }\n}\n"
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn greek_accuracy_map_is_within_the_declared_tolerances() {
+    let xs = size_parameters();
+    assert_eq!(xs.len(), 46, "size parameters");
+    let ps = particles();
+    let mu = cosines();
+    let points: Vec<(f64, usize)> = xs
+        .iter()
+        .flat_map(|&x| (0..ps.len()).map(move |j| (x, j)))
+        .collect();
+    let next = AtomicUsize::new(0);
+    let threads = std::thread::available_parallelism().map_or(1, |n| n.get());
+    let done: Vec<Vec<(usize, [f64; 4])>> = std::thread::scope(|scope| {
+        let workers: Vec<_> = (0..threads)
+            .map(|_| {
+                scope.spawn(|| {
+                    let mut mine = Vec::new();
+                    loop {
+                        let k = next.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
+                        let Some(&(x, j)) = points.get(k) else {
+                            break mine;
+                        };
+                        mine.push((k, point(x, &ps[j].0, &ps[j].1, &mu)));
+                    }
+                })
+            })
+            .collect();
+        workers
+            .into_iter()
+            .map(|w| w.join().expect("worker"))
+            .collect()
+    });
+    let mut results = vec![[0.0f64; 4]; points.len()];
+    for (k, c) in done.into_iter().flatten() {
+        results[k] = c;
+    }
+    if let Ok(path) = std::env::var("SCATRS_ACCURACY_MAP") {
+        std::fs::write(&path, to_json(&xs, &ps, &results)).expect("write the map");
+        eprintln!("wrote {path}");
+    }
+    let declared = [
+        ValidatedDomain::constant(MieOutput::PhaseMatrix),
+        ValidatedDomain::constant(MieOutput::GreekCoefficients { order: 0 }),
+        ValidatedDomain::constant(MieOutput::PhaseMatrixDerivatives),
+        ValidatedDomain::constant(MieOutput::GreekDerivatives { order: 0 }),
+    ];
+    let mut failures = Vec::new();
+    for (o, name) in OUTPUTS.iter().enumerate() {
+        let (k, worst) = results
+            .iter()
+            .enumerate()
+            .filter(|(_, c)| !(o >= 2 && c[o].is_nan()))
+            .map(|(k, c)| (k, c[o]))
+            .fold(
+                (0, 0.0),
+                |a, b| if b.1.is_nan() || b.1 > a.1 { b } else { a },
+            );
+        let (x, j) = points[k];
+        eprintln!(
+            "{name:<26} worst C = {worst:8.3} (declared {}) at x_L = {x:e}, fractions {:?}, m = {:?}",
+            declared[o], ps[j].0, ps[j].1
+        );
+        if worst.is_nan() || worst > declared[o] {
+            failures.push(*name);
+        }
+    }
+    assert!(
+        failures.is_empty(),
+        "outside the declared tolerance: {failures:?}"
+    );
+}

### Task 4: `scatrs.layered`, the Python API

**Files:**
- Create: `scatrs-py/src/layered.rs`, `python/scatrs/layered.py`, `tests/test_layered.py`
- Modify: `scatrs-py/src/{lib,mie}.rs` (sharing the helpers), `python/scatrs/{__init__.py, _scatrs/__init__.pyi}`, `tests/test_package.py`, `scatrs/src/layered/mod.rs` (`n_terms`), `docs/theory/reference-suite.md` (the new data files, whose table a docs test checks)

**Interfaces:**
- **Produces:**
  - `_scatrs.layered_{efficiencies, coefficients, amplitudes, phase_matrix, greek}` on (P, L) arrays, with derivatives per layer first;
  - `scatrs.layered.{coefficients, efficiencies, amplitudes, phase_matrix, greek, default_n_max, parameters, MAX_LAYERS}`;
  - the result classes `Layered*`, subclasses of `scatrs.mie`'s, whose `d` takes `"layer{l}.x"`, `".m_real"`, `".m_imag"` and `".m"`;
  - `scatrs::layered::n_terms`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests:** `tests/test_layered.py` (diff below).
- [ ] **Step 2: Implement** (diff below).
- [ ] **Step 3: Check.** Python reaches `394 passed`; stubtest, ruff and everything else hold.
- [ ] **Step 4: Commit:** `feat(python): scatrs.layered, the low-level API for layered spheres` (`694aad1`).

+**`layered.jsonl`** holds two records per layered sphere, for 370 particles, computed without
+scatrs's algorithm: the generator evaluates $\psi_n$ and $\xi_n$ of every layer's arguments
+and matches the fields at each interface directly, all in ball arithmetic. The particles are
+two-layer spheres of 9 pairs of indices (black carbon in sulfate, water on ice, a silica core
+in a gold-like shell, absorbing cores in a clear shell, high contrast, near unity and an air
+shell) with core radius fractions $10^{-3}$, 0.3, 0.9 and 0.999, at outer size parameters from
+$10^{-6}$ to $10^4$; three- and ten-layer particles; and 40 random particles of 2 to 8
+layers. A `,coefficients` record holds $a_n$ and $b_n$ at up to 11 orders, certified; a
+`,series` record the efficiencies, $g$, and $S_1$, $S_2$ at 9 angles, summed to
+$\lceil x + 18x^{1/3} + 32 \rceil$ terms without a tail bound, so high-precision.
+**`layered_derivatives.jsonl`** holds, for 8 particles of up to three layers, the derivatives
+of the coefficients and the efficiencies with respect to every layer's $x_l$, $\mathrm{Re}\,m_l$
+and $\mathrm{Im}\,m_l$, from five-point central differences in ball arithmetic
+(high-precision).
+
+The committed data take about 17.5 MB, within the 20 MB budget, which a test enforces.

diff --git a/python/scatrs/layered.py b/python/scatrs/layered.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..8905377
--- /dev/null
+++ b/python/scatrs/layered.py
@@ -0,0 +1,423 @@
+"""Scattering by layered spheres: a core and concentric shells (vectorized wrappers over the
+Rust core).
+
+``x`` and ``m`` have a trailing axis of layers, core first: ``x[..., l]`` is the size parameter
+2 pi r_l / wavelength of the outer surface of layer ``l`` (nondecreasing along the axis, the
+last the particle's) and ``m[..., l]`` the refractive index of layer ``l`` relative to the
+medium. Their leading axes broadcast against each other to a shape ``S``, and every result has
+that shape, extended as in :mod:`scatrs.mie`. A single layer is a sphere: its results are
+:mod:`scatrs.mie`'s, bitwise. Conventions are those of :mod:`scatrs.mie`; the outer size
+parameter normalizes the efficiencies and the phase matrix. See :doc:`/theory/layered` for the
+method and :doc:`/validation/accuracy` for the validated domain.
+
+``derivatives`` is ``True`` (every layer's ``"layer{l}.x"``, ``"layer{l}.m_real"`` and
+``"layer{l}.m_imag"``) or a sequence of those names; the result's ``d`` method returns the
+derivative with respect to one of them, or ``"layer{l}.m"`` (the complex derivative) for the
+complex outputs. The derivatives are validated up to an outer size parameter of 100.
+
+The results are those of :mod:`scatrs.mie` (:class:`~scatrs.mie.MieEfficiencies`, …), with
+``d`` accepting the layers' parameter names.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+from collections.abc import Iterable, Sequence
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from typing import Final, SupportsIndex
+
+import numpy as np
+from numpy.typing import ArrayLike, NDArray
+
+from scatrs import _args, _scatrs
+from scatrs.mie import (
+    MieAmplitudes,
+    MieCoefficients,
+    MieEfficiencies,
+    MieGreek,
+    MiePhaseMatrix,
+    _frozen,
+    _n_max,
+)
+from scatrs.mie import default_n_max as _sphere_default_n_max
+
+__all__ = [
+    "MAX_LAYERS",
+    "LayeredAmplitudes",
+    "LayeredCoefficients",
+    "LayeredEfficiencies",
+    "LayeredGreek",
+    "LayeredPhaseMatrix",
+    "amplitudes",
+    "coefficients",
+    "default_n_max",
+    "efficiencies",
+    "greek",
+    "parameters",
+    "phase_matrix",
+]
+
+MAX_LAYERS: Final[int] = _scatrs.MAX_LAYERS
+"""The largest number of layers accepted: ``2**10``."""
+
+_PARTS: Final = ("x", "m_real", "m_imag")
+_NAME: Final = re.compile(r"layer(\d+)\.(x|m_real|m_imag|m)")
+
+
+def parameters(layers: int) -> tuple[str, ...]:
+    """The parameter names of a particle of ``layers`` layers, in the order of the results."""
+    return tuple(f"layer{j}.{p}" for j in range(layers) for p in _PARTS)
+
+
+def default_n_max(x: ArrayLike) -> NDArray[np.int64]:
+    """The default number of terms: the sphere's (:func:`scatrs.mie.default_n_max`) at the outer
+    size parameter ``x[..., -1]``."""
+    xs = _args.real_array(x, "x")
+    if xs.ndim == 0 or xs.shape[-1] == 0:
+        raise ValueError("x needs a trailing axis of at least one layer")
+    return _sphere_default_n_max(xs[..., -1])
+
+
+def _inputs(
+    x: ArrayLike, m: ArrayLike
+) -> tuple[NDArray[np.float64], NDArray[np.complex128], tuple[int, ...]]:
+    """``x`` and ``m`` broadcast to one shape ``S + (L,)``, as C-contiguous (P, L) arrays."""
+    xs = _args.real_array(x, "x")
+    ms = _args.complex_array(m, "m")
+    if xs.ndim == 0 or ms.ndim == 0:
+        raise ValueError("x and m need a trailing axis of layers (one value per layer)")
+    if xs.shape[-1] != ms.shape[-1]:
+        raise ValueError(
+            f"x and m must have one value per layer: their last axes have {xs.shape[-1]} and "
+            f"{ms.shape[-1]}"
+        )
+    if xs.shape[-1] == 0:
+        raise ValueError("a layered sphere needs at least one layer")
+    xb, mb = np.broadcast_arrays(xs, ms)
+    layers = xb.shape[-1]
+    shape = xb.shape[:-1]
+    return (
+        np.ascontiguousarray(xb).reshape(-1, layers),
+        np.ascontiguousarray(mb).reshape(-1, layers),
+        shape,
+    )
+
+
+def _parameters(derivatives: bool | Sequence[str], layers: int) -> tuple[str, ...]:
+    names = parameters(layers)
+    if isinstance(derivatives, (bool, np.bool_)):
+        return names if derivatives else ()
+    if isinstance(derivatives, (str, bytes)) or not isinstance(derivatives, Iterable):
+        raise TypeError(
+            "derivatives must be a bool or a sequence of parameter names, "
+            f"not {type(derivatives).__name__}"
+        )
+    out = tuple(derivatives)
+    for name in out:
+        if name not in names:
+            raise ValueError(f"unknown parameter {name!r}; expected one of {names}")
+    return out
+
+
+def _index(params: tuple[str, ...], param: str, real: str | None = None) -> tuple[int, int]:
+    """(layer, part) of ``param`` (part 0, 1, 2 for x, Re m, Im m; 3 for the complex d/dm),
+    if it was requested; ``real`` names real outputs, which have no complex derivative."""
+    match = _NAME.fullmatch(param)
+    if match is not None:
+        layer, part = int(match[1]), match[2]
+        requested = param in params or (
+            part == "m" and {f"layer{layer}.m_real", f"layer{layer}.m_imag"} & set(params)
+        )
+        if requested:
+            if part == "m" and real is not None:
+                raise ValueError(f"{real} are real: use 'layer{layer}.m_real' or '.m_imag'")
+            return layer, ("x", "m_real", "m_imag", "m").index(part)
+    raise ValueError(f"no derivative with respect to {param!r}; computed: {params}")
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
+class LayeredEfficiencies(MieEfficiencies):
+    """:class:`~scatrs.mie.MieEfficiencies` of layered spheres, per unit geometric cross section
+    of the outer surface; ``d`` takes the layers' parameter names."""
+
+    def d(self, param: str) -> MieEfficiencies:
+        layer, p = _index(self.params, param, "efficiencies")
+        assert self._jacobian is not None
+        return MieEfficiencies(*self._jacobian[layer, p])
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
+class LayeredCoefficients(MieCoefficients):
+    """:class:`~scatrs.mie.MieCoefficients` of layered spheres; ``d`` takes the layers'
+    parameter names (``"layer{l}.m"``: the complex derivative d/dm_l)."""
+
+    def d(self, param: str) -> MieCoefficients:
+        layer, p = _index(self.params, param)
+        assert self._derivatives is not None
+        da_dx, da_dm, db_dx, db_dm = self._derivatives[layer]
+        if p == 0:
+            return MieCoefficients(da_dx, db_dx, self.n_terms)
+        if p == 2:
+            return MieCoefficients(_frozen(1j * da_dm), _frozen(1j * db_dm), self.n_terms)
+        return MieCoefficients(da_dm, db_dm, self.n_terms)
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
+class LayeredAmplitudes(MieAmplitudes):
+    """:class:`~scatrs.mie.MieAmplitudes` of layered spheres; ``d`` takes the layers'
+    parameter names (``"layer{l}.m"``: the complex derivative d/dm_l)."""
+
+    def d(self, param: str) -> MieAmplitudes:
+        layer, p = _index(self.params, param)
+        assert self._derivatives is not None
+        ds1_dx, ds2_dx, ds1_dm, ds2_dm = self._derivatives[layer]
+        if p == 0:
+            return MieAmplitudes(ds1_dx, ds2_dx)
+        if p == 2:
+            return MieAmplitudes(_frozen(1j * ds1_dm), _frozen(1j * ds2_dm))
+        return MieAmplitudes(ds1_dm, ds2_dm)
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
+class LayeredPhaseMatrix(MiePhaseMatrix):
+    """:class:`~scatrs.mie.MiePhaseMatrix` of layered spheres; ``d`` takes the layers'
+    parameter names."""
+
+    def d(self, param: str) -> MiePhaseMatrix:
+        layer, p = _index(self.params, param, "phase-matrix elements")
+        assert self._derivatives is not None
+        return MiePhaseMatrix(*self._derivatives[layer, p])
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
+class LayeredGreek(MieGreek):
+    """:class:`~scatrs.mie.MieGreek` of layered spheres; ``d`` takes the layers' parameter
+    names."""
+
+    def d(self, param: str) -> MieGreek:
+        layer, p = _index(self.params, param, "Greek coefficients")
+        assert self._derivatives is not None
+        return MieGreek(*self._derivatives[layer, p], self.n_terms)
+
+
+_SHARED_DOC = """
+    Parameters
+    ----------
+    x : array_like
+        Size parameters of the interfaces, 2 pi r_l / wavelength, along a trailing axis of
+        layers (core first, nondecreasing).
+    m : array_like
+        Refractive indices of the layers relative to the medium (real or complex), along the
+        same trailing axis; the leading axes broadcast against those of ``x``.
+    {extra}n_max : int, optional
+        The number of terms; by default the sphere's at the outer size parameter. Fewer terms
+        than the default are outside the validated domain.
+    derivatives : bool or sequence of str, optional
+        Also compute derivatives with respect to these of ``"layer{{l}}.x"``,
+        ``"layer{{l}}.m_real"`` and ``"layer{{l}}.m_imag"`` (``True`` for all); read them with the
+        result's ``d`` method. Validated up to an outer size parameter of 100.
+    allow_unvalidated : bool, optional
+        Compute outside the validated domain instead of raising
+        :class:`~scatrs.errors.OutsideValidatedDomainError` (see
+        :doc:`/validation/accuracy`).
+
+    Raises
+    ------
+    InvalidInputError
+        For invalid inputs (as :mod:`scatrs.mie`'s, for every layer), size parameters that
+        decrease from the core out, or more than ``MAX_LAYERS`` layers{extra_raise}. The
+        message names the first failing particle (in C order).
+    OutsideValidatedDomainError
+        For valid inputs outside the validated domain, unless ``allow_unvalidated``.
+    TypeError
+        For non-numeric ``x`` or ``m``, or arguments of the wrong type{extra_type}.
+    ValueError
+        For an unknown parameter name, no layer axis, last axes of different lengths, or
+        (from numpy) leading shapes that do not broadcast.
+"""
+
+
+def efficiencies(
+    x: ArrayLike,
+    m: ArrayLike,
+    *,
+    n_max: SupportsIndex | None = None,
+    derivatives: bool | Sequence[str] = False,
+    allow_unvalidated: bool = False,
+) -> LayeredEfficiencies:
+    xb, mb, shape = _inputs(x, m)
+    params = _parameters(derivatives, xb.shape[1])
+    values, jacobian = _scatrs.layered_efficiencies(
+        xb, mb, _n_max(n_max), bool(params), _args.flag(allow_unvalidated, "allow_unvalidated")
+    )
+    values, jacobian = _frozen(values), _frozen(jacobian)
+    fields = [v.reshape(shape) for v in values]
+    jac = None if jacobian is None else jacobian.reshape((*jacobian.shape[:3], *shape))
+    return LayeredEfficiencies(*fields, params=params, _jacobian=jac)
+
+
+efficiencies.__doc__ = """Efficiencies and asymmetry parameter of layered spheres.
+
+    Returns
+    -------
+    LayeredEfficiencies
+        Each field has the broadcast leading shape ``S``.
+""" + _SHARED_DOC.format(extra="", extra_raise="", extra_type="")
+
+
+def coefficients(
+    x: ArrayLike,
+    m: ArrayLike,
+    *,
+    n_max: SupportsIndex | None = None,
+    derivatives: bool | Sequence[str] = False,
+    allow_unvalidated: bool = False,
+) -> LayeredCoefficients:
+    xb, mb, shape = _inputs(x, m)
+    params = _parameters(derivatives, xb.shape[1])
+    terms, a, b, d = _scatrs.layered_coefficients(
+        xb, mb, _n_max(n_max), bool(params), _args.flag(allow_unvalidated, "allow_unvalidated")
+    )
+    a, b, d = _frozen(a), _frozen(b), _frozen(d)
+    width = a.shape[1]
+    n_terms = _frozen(terms.astype(np.int64)).reshape(shape)
+    dd = None if d is None else d.reshape((*d.shape[:2], *shape, width))
+    return LayeredCoefficients(
+        a.reshape((*shape, width)), b.reshape((*shape, width)), n_terms, params, dd
+    )
+
+
+coefficients.__doc__ = """Mie coefficients aₙ and bₙ of layered spheres.
+
+    Returns
+    -------
+    LayeredCoefficients
+        Shape ``S + (N,)``, zero-padded to the largest number of terms ``N``.
+""" + _SHARED_DOC.format(extra="", extra_raise="", extra_type="")
+
+
+def amplitudes(
+    x: ArrayLike,
+    m: ArrayLike,
+    mu: ArrayLike,
+    *,
+    n_max: SupportsIndex | None = None,
+    derivatives: bool | Sequence[str] = False,
+    allow_unvalidated: bool = False,
+) -> LayeredAmplitudes:
+    xb, mb, shape = _inputs(x, m)
+    params = _parameters(derivatives, xb.shape[1])
+    mus = _args.real_array(mu, "mu")
+    s, d = _scatrs.layered_amplitudes(
+        xb,
+        mb,
+        mus.reshape(mus.shape),
+        _n_max(n_max),
+        bool(params),
+        _args.flag(allow_unvalidated, "allow_unvalidated"),
+    )
+    s, d = _frozen(s), _frozen(d)
+    out = (*shape, *mus.shape)
+    dd = None if d is None else d.reshape((*d.shape[:2], *out))
+    return LayeredAmplitudes(s[0].reshape(out), s[1].reshape(out), params, dd)
+
+
+amplitudes.__doc__ = """Amplitude scattering functions S₁ and S₂ of layered spheres.
+
+    Returns
+    -------
+    LayeredAmplitudes
+        Shape ``S + mu.shape``.
+""" + _SHARED_DOC.format(
+    extra="mu : array_like\n        Cosines of the scattering angle, in [-1, 1].\n    ",
+    extra_raise=", ``mu`` outside [-1, 1] or NaN",
+    extra_type="",
+)
+
+
+def phase_matrix(
+    x: ArrayLike,
+    m: ArrayLike,
+    mu: ArrayLike,
+    *,
+    normalized: bool = True,
+    n_max: SupportsIndex | None = None,
+    derivatives: bool | Sequence[str] = False,
+    allow_unvalidated: bool = False,
+) -> LayeredPhaseMatrix:
+    xb, mb, shape = _inputs(x, m)
+    params = _parameters(derivatives, xb.shape[1])
+    mus = _args.real_array(mu, "mu")
+    f, d = _scatrs.layered_phase_matrix(
+        xb,
+        mb,
+        mus.reshape(mus.shape),
+        _n_max(n_max),
+        bool(params),
+        _args.flag(allow_unvalidated, "allow_unvalidated"),
+        _args.flag(normalized, "normalized"),
+    )
+    f, d = _frozen(f), _frozen(d)
+    out = (*shape, *mus.shape)
+    dd = None if d is None else d.reshape((*d.shape[:3], *out))
+    return LayeredPhaseMatrix(*(v.reshape(out) for v in f), params=params, _derivatives=dd)
+
+
+phase_matrix.__doc__ = """The phase matrix F11, F12, F33, F34 of layered spheres.
+
+    Returns
+    -------
+    LayeredPhaseMatrix
+        Shape ``S + mu.shape``.
+""" + _SHARED_DOC.format(
+    extra="mu : array_like\n        Cosines of the scattering angle, in [-1, 1].\n"
+    "    normalized : bool, optional\n        Divide by ``q_sca`` (the default), so that F11"
+    " averages to 1.\n    ",
+    extra_raise=", ``mu`` outside [-1, 1] or NaN",
+    extra_type="",
+)
+
+
+def greek(
+    x: ArrayLike,
+    m: ArrayLike,
+    order: SupportsIndex | None = None,
+    *,
+    normalized: bool = True,
+    n_max: SupportsIndex | None = None,
+    derivatives: bool | Sequence[str] = False,
+    allow_unvalidated: bool = False,
+) -> LayeredGreek:
+    xb, mb, shape = _inputs(x, m)
+    params = _parameters(derivatives, xb.shape[1])
+    terms, g, d = _scatrs.layered_greek(
+        xb,
+        mb,
+        None if order is None else _args.size(order, "order"),
+        _n_max(n_max),
+        bool(params),
+        _args.flag(allow_unvalidated, "allow_unvalidated"),
+        _args.flag(normalized, "normalized"),
+    )
+    g, d = _frozen(g), _frozen(d)
+    width = g.shape[-1]
+    out = (*shape, width)
+    n_terms = _frozen(terms.astype(np.int64)).reshape(shape)
+    dd = None if d is None else d.reshape((*d.shape[:3], *out))
+    return LayeredGreek(*(v.reshape(out) for v in g), n_terms, params, dd)
+
+
+greek.__doc__ = """Greek expansion coefficients of the phase matrix of layered spheres, exact up to
+    rounding, as :func:`scatrs.mie.greek`.
+
+    Returns
+    -------
+    LayeredGreek
+        Shape ``S + (L + 1,)``.
+""" + _SHARED_DOC.format(
+    extra="order : int, optional\n        The highest order L; by default ``2 n_terms`` of the"
+    " largest particle.\n    normalized : bool, optional\n        Normalize ``alpha1[..., 0]`` to"
+    " 1 (the default), or to ``q_sca``.\n    ",
+    extra_raise=", a negative ``order`` or one above ``MAX_GREEK_ORDER``",
+    extra_type="",
+)
diff --git a/scatrs-py/src/layered.rs b/scatrs-py/src/layered.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..38fc005
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scatrs-py/src/layered.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,570 @@
+//! Bindings for `scatrs::layered`.
+//!
+//! Every function takes `x` (float64) and `m` (complex128) arrays of one shape (P, L): one
+//! layered sphere per row, its interfaces' size parameters and its layers' indices, core first,
+//! C-contiguous (`scatrs.layered` makes them so). The outputs have one row per particle, as in
+//! `mie.rs`, whose helpers and bounds (`MAX_MIE_VALUES`) these share; derivatives come per
+//! layer, first. The GIL is released while computing.
+#![allow(clippy::needless_range_loop)] // particles are rows of x and m, indexed with the outputs
+
+use numpy::ndarray::{ArrayD, ArrayView2, Ix2, IxDyn};
+use numpy::{Complex64, IntoPyArray, PyArray1, PyArrayDyn};
+use pyo3::exceptions::PyValueError;
+use pyo3::prelude::*;
+use scatrs::layered::{Layered, LayeredSolution, n_terms};
+use scatrs::mie::{
+    AmplitudeBuffers, AngularTable, GreekCoefficients, MAX_GREEK_ORDER, MAX_MIE_ORDER, MieOptions,
+};
+
+use crate::errors::to_pyerr;
+use crate::mie::{
+    Failure, MAX_TABLE_VALUES, at, borrow, complex_values, cosines, greek_arrays, invalid,
+    normalization, phase_buffers, real_values, shape_error, zeros,
+};
+
+/// The inputs as (P, L) views and the options, after checking their shapes and `n_max`.
+fn prepare<'a>(
+    py: Python<'_>,
+    x: &'a ArrayView2<'a, f64>,
+    m: &'a ArrayView2<'a, Complex64>,
+    n_max: Option<usize>,
+    allow_unvalidated: bool,
+) -> PyResult<MieOptions> {
+    if x.shape() != m.shape() {
+        return Err(PyValueError::new_err(format!(
+            "x and m must have one shape (broadcast them first), got {:?} and {:?}",
+            x.shape(),
+            m.shape()
+        )));
+    }
+    if !(x.is_standard_layout() && m.is_standard_layout()) {
+        return Err(PyValueError::new_err("x and m must be C-contiguous"));
+    }
+    let mut opts = MieOptions::new().with_allow_unvalidated(allow_unvalidated);
+    if let Some(n) = n_max {
+        if n == 0 || n > MAX_MIE_ORDER {
+            return Err(to_pyerr(
+                py,
+                invalid(format!(
+                    "the number of terms must be in [1, {MAX_MIE_ORDER}], got {n}"
+                )),
+            ));
+        }
+        opts = opts.with_n_max(n);
+    }
+    Ok(opts)
+}
+
+/// Particle `i`: its size parameters and indices.
+fn row<'a>(
+    x: &'a ArrayView2<'_, f64>,
+    m: &'a ArrayView2<'_, Complex64>,
+    i: usize,
+) -> (&'a [f64], &'a [Complex64]) {
+    (
+        x.row(i).to_slice().expect("C-contiguous"),
+        m.row(i).to_slice().expect("C-contiguous"),
+    )
+}
+
+/// Checks every particle and returns the largest number of terms, before anything is allocated.
+fn largest_n_terms(
+    x: &ArrayView2<'_, f64>,
+    m: &ArrayView2<'_, Complex64>,
+    opts: &MieOptions,
+) -> scatrs::Result<usize> {
+    let mut most = 0;
+    for i in 0..x.nrows() {
+        let (xi, mi) = row(x, m, i);
+        most = most.max(n_terms(xi, mi, opts).map_err(at(i))?);
+    }
+    Ok(most)
+}
+
+fn solve<'w>(
+    lay: &'w mut Layered<f64>,
+    i: usize,
+    x: &[f64],
+    m: &[Complex64],
+    opts: &MieOptions,
+    derivatives: bool,
+) -> scatrs::Result<LayeredSolution<'w, f64>> {
+    if derivatives {
+        lay.compute_with_derivatives(x, m, opts)
+    } else {
+        lay.compute(x, m, opts)
+    }
+    .map_err(at(i))
+}
+
+fn views<'a>(
+    x: &'a numpy::PyReadonlyArrayDyn<'_, f64>,
+    m: &'a numpy::PyReadonlyArrayDyn<'_, Complex64>,
+) -> PyResult<(ArrayView2<'a, f64>, ArrayView2<'a, Complex64>)> {
+    let two = |e| PyValueError::new_err(format!("x and m must be two-dimensional: {e}"));
+    Ok((
+        x.as_array().into_dimensionality::<Ix2>().map_err(two)?,
+        m.as_array().into_dimensionality::<Ix2>().map_err(two)?,
+    ))
+}
+
+fn array<'py, T: numpy::Element>(
+    py: Python<'py>,
+    shape: &[usize],
+    v: Vec<T>,
+) -> PyResult<Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<T>>> {
+    Ok(ArrayD::from_shape_vec(IxDyn(shape), v)
+        .map_err(shape_error)?
+        .into_pyarray(py))
+}
+
+type Arrays<'py, T> = (Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<T>>, Option<Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<T>>>);
+
+/// Efficiencies (5, P) in the order Q_ext, Q_sca, Q_abs, Q_back, g, and with `derivatives`
+/// their Jacobian (L, 3, 5, P) with respect to each layer's x, Re m and Im m.
+#[pyfunction]
+pub fn layered_efficiencies<'py>(
+    py: Python<'py>,
+    x: Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<f64>>,
+    m: Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<Complex64>>,
+    n_max: Option<usize>,
+    derivatives: bool,
+    allow_unvalidated: bool,
+) -> PyResult<Arrays<'py, f64>> {
+    let (x, m) = (borrow(&x, "x")?, borrow(&m, "m")?);
+    let (xv, mv) = views(&x, &m)?;
+    let opts = prepare(py, &xv, &mv, n_max, allow_unvalidated)?;
+    let (len, layers) = xv.dim();
+    let per = if derivatives { 5 + 15 * layers } else { 5 };
+    real_values("the efficiencies", len.checked_mul(per), derivatives)
+        .map_err(|e| to_pyerr(py, e))?;
+    let (values, jacobian) = py
+        .detach(|| -> Result<_, Failure> {
+            let mut values = zeros::<f64>(5 * len, "the efficiencies")?;
+            let mut jac = zeros::<f64>(
+                if derivatives { 15 * layers * len } else { 0 },
+                "the Jacobian",
+            )?;
+            let mut lay = Layered::<f64>::new();
+            for i in 0..len {
+                let (xi, mi) = row(&xv, &mv, i);
+                let s = solve(&mut lay, i, xi, mi, &opts, derivatives)?;
+                let q = s.efficiencies();
+                for (k, v) in [q.q_ext, q.q_sca, q.q_abs, q.q_back, q.g]
+                    .into_iter()
+                    .enumerate()
+                {
+                    values[k * len + i] = v;
+                }
+                for (l, j) in s
+                    .efficiencies_jacobian()
+                    .unwrap_or_default()
+                    .iter()
+                    .enumerate()
+                {
+                    for (p, d) in [j.x, j.m_re, j.m_im].into_iter().enumerate() {
+                        for (k, v) in [d.q_ext, d.q_sca, d.q_abs, d.q_back, d.g]
+                            .into_iter()
+                            .enumerate()
+                        {
+                            jac[((l * 3 + p) * 5 + k) * len + i] = v;
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            Ok((values, jac))
+        })
+        .map_err(|f| f.into_pyerr(py))?;
+    let jacobian = if derivatives {
+        Some(array(py, &[layers, 3, 5, len], jacobian)?)
+    } else {
+        None
+    };
+    Ok((array(py, &[5, len], values)?, jacobian))
+}
+
+type CoefficientArrays<'py> = (
+    Bound<'py, PyArray1<u64>>,
+    Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<Complex64>>,
+    Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<Complex64>>,
+    Option<Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<Complex64>>>,
+);
+
+/// The number of terms of each particle (P), aₙ and bₙ (P, N) zero-padded to the largest N, and
+/// with `derivatives` ∂a/∂x_l, da/dm_l, ∂b/∂x_l, db/dm_l per layer (L, 4, P, N).
+#[pyfunction]
+pub fn layered_coefficients<'py>(
+    py: Python<'py>,
+    x: Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<f64>>,
+    m: Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<Complex64>>,
+    n_max: Option<usize>,
+    derivatives: bool,
+    allow_unvalidated: bool,
+) -> PyResult<CoefficientArrays<'py>> {
+    let (x, m) = (borrow(&x, "x")?, borrow(&m, "m")?);
+    let (xv, mv) = views(&x, &m)?;
+    let opts = prepare(py, &xv, &mv, n_max, allow_unvalidated)?;
+    let (len, layers) = xv.dim();
+    let arrays = if derivatives { 2 + 4 * layers } else { 2 };
+    complex_values("the coefficients", len.checked_mul(arrays), derivatives)
+        .map_err(|e| to_pyerr(py, e))?;
+    let (terms, a, b, d, width) = py
+        .detach(|| -> Result<_, Failure> {
+            let width = largest_n_terms(&xv, &mv, &opts)?;
+            let count = len.checked_mul(width).and_then(|v| v.checked_mul(arrays));
+            let per_array = complex_values("the coefficients", count, derivatives)? / arrays;
+            let mut terms = zeros::<u64>(len, "the numbers of terms")?;
+            let mut a = zeros::<Complex64>(per_array, "the coefficients")?;
+            let mut b = zeros::<Complex64>(per_array, "the coefficients")?;
+            let mut d = zeros::<Complex64>(
+                if derivatives {
+                    4 * layers * per_array
+                } else {
+                    0
+                },
+                "derivatives",
+            )?;
+            let mut lay = Layered::<f64>::new();
+            for i in 0..len {
+                let (xi, mi) = row(&xv, &mv, i);
+                let s = solve(&mut lay, i, xi, mi, &opts, derivatives)?;
+                if s.n_terms() > width {
+                    return Err(
+                        invalid(format!("element {i}: x or m changed during the call")).into(),
+                    );
+                }
+                terms[i] = s.n_terms() as u64;
+                let r = i * width..i * width + s.n_terms();
+                a[r.clone()].copy_from_slice(s.a());
+                b[r.clone()].copy_from_slice(s.b());
+                for l in 0..if derivatives { layers } else { 0 } {
+                    let dc = s.derivatives(l).expect("computed");
+                    for (k, src) in [dc.da_dx, dc.da_dm, dc.db_dx, dc.db_dm]
+                        .into_iter()
+                        .enumerate()
+                    {
+                        let off = (4 * l + k) * per_array;
+                        d[off + r.start..off + r.end].copy_from_slice(src);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            Ok((terms, a, b, d, width))
+        })
+        .map_err(|f| f.into_pyerr(py))?;
+    let d = if derivatives {
+        Some(array(py, &[layers, 4, len, width], d)?)
+    } else {
+        None
+    };
+    Ok((
+        PyArray1::from_vec(py, terms),
+        array(py, &[len, width], a)?,
+        array(py, &[len, width], b)?,
+        d,
+    ))
+}
+
+/// An angular table at the cosines `mu` for every particle, if there are several to share it
+/// and it holds at most `MAX_TABLE_VALUES` (as `mie.rs` builds one).
+fn angular_table(
+    x: &ArrayView2<'_, f64>,
+    m: &ArrayView2<'_, Complex64>,
+    opts: &MieOptions,
+    mu: &[f64],
+) -> scatrs::Result<Option<AngularTable<f64>>> {
+    if x.nrows() < 2 || mu.is_empty() {
+        return Ok(None);
+    }
+    let most = largest_n_terms(x, m, opts)?;
+    if most
+        .checked_mul(2 * mu.len())
+        .is_none_or(|v| v > MAX_TABLE_VALUES)
+    {
+        return Ok(None);
+    }
+    AngularTable::new(mu, most).map(Some)
+}
+
+/// S₁ and S₂ (2, P, n_mu) at the cosines `mu`, and with `derivatives` ∂S₁/∂x_l, ∂S₂/∂x_l,
+/// dS₁/dm_l, dS₂/dm_l per layer (L, 4, P, n_mu).
+#[pyfunction]
+#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
+pub fn layered_amplitudes<'py>(
+    py: Python<'py>,
+    x: Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<f64>>,
+    m: Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<Complex64>>,
+    mu: Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<f64>>,
+    n_max: Option<usize>,
+    derivatives: bool,
+    allow_unvalidated: bool,
+) -> PyResult<Arrays<'py, Complex64>> {
+    let (x, m, mu) = (borrow(&x, "x")?, borrow(&m, "m")?, borrow(&mu, "mu")?);
+    let (xv, mv) = views(&x, &m)?;
+    let opts = prepare(py, &xv, &mv, n_max, allow_unvalidated)?;
+    let mu = cosines(py, &mu)?;
+    let ((len, layers), n_mu) = (xv.dim(), mu.len());
+    let arrays = if derivatives { 2 + 4 * layers } else { 2 };
+    let count = len
+        .checked_mul(n_mu.max(1))
+        .and_then(|v| v.checked_mul(arrays));
+    complex_values("the amplitudes", count, derivatives).map_err(|e| to_pyerr(py, e))?;
+    let per_array = len * n_mu;
+    let (s, d) = py
+        .detach(|| -> Result<_, Failure> {
+            let mut s = zeros::<Complex64>(2 * per_array, "the amplitudes")?;
+            let mut d = zeros::<Complex64>(
+                if derivatives {
+                    4 * layers * per_array
+                } else {
+                    0
+                },
+                "derivatives",
+            )?;
+            let table = angular_table(&xv, &mv, &opts, &mu)?;
+            let mut lay = Layered::<f64>::new();
+            let (s1, s2) = s.split_at_mut(per_array);
+            for i in 0..len {
+                let (xi, mi) = row(&xv, &mv, i);
+                if n_mu == 0 {
+                    n_terms(xi, mi, &opts).map_err(at(i))?;
+                    continue;
+                }
+                let sol = solve(&mut lay, i, xi, mi, &opts, derivatives)?;
+                let r = i * n_mu..(i + 1) * n_mu;
+                let values = AmplitudeBuffers {
+                    s1: &mut s1[r.clone()],
+                    s2: &mut s2[r.clone()],
+                };
+                match &table {
+                    Some(t) => sol.amplitudes_from_table(t, values),
+                    None => sol.amplitudes(&mu, values),
+                }
+                .map_err(at(i))?;
+                for l in 0..if derivatives { layers } else { 0 } {
+                    let block = &mut d[4 * l * per_array..4 * (l + 1) * per_array];
+                    let (dx, dm) = block.split_at_mut(2 * per_array);
+                    let (dx1, dx2) = dx.split_at_mut(per_array);
+                    let (dm1, dm2) = dm.split_at_mut(per_array);
+                    // The values again, into scratch: they were written above.
+                    let z = Complex64::default();
+                    let (mut v1, mut v2) = (vec![z; n_mu], vec![z; n_mu]);
+                    let values = AmplitudeBuffers {
+                        s1: &mut v1,
+                        s2: &mut v2,
+                    };
+                    let d_dx = AmplitudeBuffers {
+                        s1: &mut dx1[r.clone()],
+                        s2: &mut dx2[r.clone()],
+                    };
+                    let d_dm = AmplitudeBuffers {
+                        s1: &mut dm1[r.clone()],
+                        s2: &mut dm2[r.clone()],
+                    };
+                    match &table {
+                        Some(t) => {
+                            sol.amplitudes_with_derivatives_from_table(t, l, values, d_dx, d_dm)
+                        }
+                        None => sol.amplitudes_with_derivatives(&mu, l, values, d_dx, d_dm),
+                    }
+                    .map_err(at(i))?;
+                }
+            }
+            Ok((s, d))
+        })
+        .map_err(|f| f.into_pyerr(py))?;
+    let d = if derivatives {
+        Some(array(py, &[layers, 4, len, n_mu], d)?)
+    } else {
+        None
+    };
+    Ok((array(py, &[2, len, n_mu], s)?, d))
+}
+
+/// F₁₁, F₁₂, F₃₃, F₃₄ (4, P, n_mu) at the cosines `mu`, normalized or not, and with
+/// `derivatives` their derivatives with respect to each layer's x, Re m and Im m
+/// (L, 3, 4, P, n_mu).
+#[pyfunction]
+#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
+pub fn layered_phase_matrix<'py>(
+    py: Python<'py>,
+    x: Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<f64>>,
+    m: Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<Complex64>>,
+    mu: Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<f64>>,
+    n_max: Option<usize>,
+    derivatives: bool,
+    allow_unvalidated: bool,
+    normalized: bool,
+) -> PyResult<Arrays<'py, f64>> {
+    let (x, m, mu) = (borrow(&x, "x")?, borrow(&m, "m")?, borrow(&mu, "mu")?);
+    let (xv, mv) = views(&x, &m)?;
+    let opts = prepare(py, &xv, &mv, n_max, allow_unvalidated)?;
+    let mu = cosines(py, &mu)?;
+    let ((len, layers), n_mu) = (xv.dim(), mu.len());
+    let arrays = if derivatives { 4 + 12 * layers } else { 4 };
+    let count = len
+        .checked_mul(n_mu.max(1))
+        .and_then(|v| v.checked_mul(arrays));
+    real_values("the phase matrix", count, derivatives).map_err(|e| to_pyerr(py, e))?;
+    let per_array = len * n_mu;
+    let norm = normalization(normalized);
+    let (f, d) = py
+        .detach(|| -> Result<_, Failure> {
+            let mut f = zeros::<f64>(4 * per_array, "the phase matrix")?;
+            let mut d = zeros::<f64>(
+                if derivatives {
+                    12 * layers * per_array
+                } else {
+                    0
+                },
+                "derivatives",
+            )?;
+            let table = angular_table(&xv, &mv, &opts, &mu)?;
+            let mut lay = Layered::<f64>::new();
+            for i in 0..len {
+                let (xi, mi) = row(&xv, &mv, i);
+                if n_mu == 0 {
+                    n_terms(xi, mi, &opts).map_err(at(i))?;
+                    continue;
+                }
+                let sol = solve(&mut lay, i, xi, mi, &opts, derivatives)?;
+                let r = i * n_mu..(i + 1) * n_mu;
+                match &table {
+                    Some(t) => sol.phase_matrix_from_table(
+                        t,
+                        norm,
+                        phase_buffers(&mut f, per_array, r.clone()),
+                    ),
+                    None => {
+                        sol.phase_matrix(&mu, norm, phase_buffers(&mut f, per_array, r.clone()))
+                    }
+                }
+                .map_err(at(i))?;
+                for l in 0..if derivatives { layers } else { 0 } {
+                    let block = &mut d[12 * l * per_array..12 * (l + 1) * per_array];
+                    let (dx, rest) = block.split_at_mut(4 * per_array);
+                    let (dr, di) = rest.split_at_mut(4 * per_array);
+                    let mut v = vec![0.0; 4 * n_mu];
+                    let values = phase_buffers(&mut v, n_mu, 0..n_mu);
+                    let dd = [
+                        phase_buffers(dx, per_array, r.clone()),
+                        phase_buffers(dr, per_array, r.clone()),
+                        phase_buffers(di, per_array, r.clone()),
+                    ];
+                    match &table {
+                        Some(t) => {
+                            sol.phase_matrix_with_derivatives_from_table(t, l, norm, values, dd)
+                        }
+                        None => sol.phase_matrix_with_derivatives(&mu, l, norm, values, dd),
+                    }
+                    .map_err(at(i))?;
+                }
+            }
+            Ok((f, d))
+        })
+        .map_err(|f| f.into_pyerr(py))?;
+    let d = if derivatives {
+        Some(array(py, &[layers, 3, 4, len, n_mu], d)?)
+    } else {
+        None
+    };
+    Ok((array(py, &[4, len, n_mu], f)?, d))
+}
+
+type GreekArrays<'py> = (
+    Bound<'py, PyArray1<u64>>,
+    Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<f64>>,
+    Option<Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<f64>>>,
+);
+
+/// The number of terms of each particle (P), the Greek coefficients (6, P, L + 1) up to `order`
+/// (by default the largest 2N), normalized or not, and with `derivatives` their derivatives
+/// with respect to each layer's x, Re m and Im m (layers, 3, 6, P, L + 1).
+#[pyfunction]
+#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
+pub fn layered_greek<'py>(
+    py: Python<'py>,
+    x: Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<f64>>,
+    m: Bound<'py, PyArrayDyn<Complex64>>,
+    order: Option<usize>,
+    n_max: Option<usize>,
+    derivatives: bool,
+    allow_unvalidated: bool,
+    normalized: bool,
+) -> PyResult<GreekArrays<'py>> {
+    let (x, m) = (borrow(&x, "x")?, borrow(&m, "m")?);
+    let (xv, mv) = views(&x, &m)?;
+    let opts = prepare(py, &xv, &mv, n_max, allow_unvalidated)?;
+    if let Some(l) = order.filter(|&l| l > MAX_GREEK_ORDER) {
+        return Err(to_pyerr(
+            py,
+            invalid(format!(
+                "the Greek order must be at most {MAX_GREEK_ORDER}, got {l}"
+            )),
+        ));
+    }
+    let (len, layers) = xv.dim();
+    let arrays = if derivatives { 6 + 18 * layers } else { 6 };
+    real_values(
+        "the Greek coefficients",
+        len.checked_mul(arrays),
+        derivatives,
+    )
+    .map_err(|e| to_pyerr(py, e))?;
+    let norm = normalization(normalized);
+    let (terms, g, d, width) = py
+        .detach(|| -> Result<_, Failure> {
+            let most = largest_n_terms(&xv, &mv, &opts)?;
+            let width = order.unwrap_or(2 * most) + 1;
+            let count = len.checked_mul(width).and_then(|v| v.checked_mul(arrays));
+            let per_array = real_values("the Greek coefficients", count, derivatives)? / arrays;
+            let mut terms = zeros::<u64>(len, "the numbers of terms")?;
+            let mut g = zeros::<f64>(6 * per_array, "the Greek coefficients")?;
+            let mut d = zeros::<f64>(
+                if derivatives {
+                    18 * layers * per_array
+                } else {
+                    0
+                },
+                "derivatives",
+            )?;
+            let mut lay = Layered::<f64>::new();
+            for i in 0..len {
+                let (xi, mi) = row(&xv, &mv, i);
+                let s = solve(&mut lay, i, xi, mi, &opts, derivatives)?;
+                if order.is_none() && s.n_terms() > most {
+                    return Err(
+                        invalid(format!("element {i}: x or m changed during the call")).into(),
+                    );
+                }
+                terms[i] = s.n_terms() as u64;
+                let r = i * width..(i + 1) * width;
+                let put = |out: &mut [f64], first: usize, c: &GreekCoefficients<f64>| {
+                    for (k, src) in greek_arrays(c).into_iter().enumerate() {
+                        let off = (first + k) * per_array;
+                        out[off + r.start..off + r.end].copy_from_slice(src);
+                    }
+                };
+                put(&mut g, 0, &s.greek(Some(width - 1), norm).map_err(at(i))?);
+                for l in 0..if derivatives { layers } else { 0 } {
+                    let (_, jac) = s
+                        .greek_with_derivatives(Some(width - 1), l, norm)
+                        .map_err(at(i))?;
+                    for (p, c) in jac.iter().enumerate() {
+                        put(&mut d, 6 * (3 * l + p), c);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            Ok((terms, g, d, width))
+        })
+        .map_err(|f| f.into_pyerr(py))?;
+    let d = if derivatives {
+        Some(array(py, &[layers, 3, 6, len, width], d)?)
+    } else {
+        None
+    };
+    Ok((
+        PyArray1::from_vec(py, terms),
+        array(py, &[6, len, width], g)?,
+        d,
+    ))
+}

 );

-fn greek_arrays(g: &GreekCoefficients<f64>) -> [&[f64]; 6] {

+++ b/scatrs/src/layered/mod.rs

+/// The number of terms [`Layered::compute`] would use for `f64` at (`x`, `m`) with `opts`

+}

diff --git a/tests/test_layered.py b/tests/test_layered.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..3ced228
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_layered.py
@@ -0,0 +1,129 @@
+"""scatrs.layered: shapes, names, the identities with scatrs.mie, derivatives and errors."""
+
+import numpy as np
+import pytest
+
+from scatrs import InvalidInputError, OutsideValidatedDomainError, layered, mie
+
+X = np.array([[0.7, 1.3], [3.0, 8.0], [10.0, 12.0]])
+M = np.array([1.95 + 0.79j, 1.43 + 1e-8j])
+MU = np.array([-1.0, -0.3, 0.4, 1.0])
+
+
+def test_shapes_broadcast_over_the_leading_axes():
+    x = X.reshape(3, 1, 2)
+    m = np.stack([M, [1.5 + 0.01j, 1.33]])  # (2, 2)
+    q = layered.efficiencies(x, m)
+    assert q.q_ext.shape == (3, 2)
+    s = layered.amplitudes(x, m, MU.reshape(2, 2))
+    assert s.s1.shape == (3, 2, 2, 2)
+    c = layered.coefficients(x, m)
+    assert c.a.shape == (3, 2, c.n_terms.max()) and c.n_terms.shape == (3, 2)
+    g = layered.greek(x, m, 6)
+    assert g.alpha1.shape == (3, 2, 7)
+    f = layered.phase_matrix(x, m, MU)
+    assert f.f11.shape == (3, 2, 4)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize(("x", "m"), [(0.3, 1.33), (5.0, 1.5 + 0.01j), (40.0, 2.5 + 1j)])
+def test_one_layer_is_the_sphere_bitwise(x, m):
+    def same(u, v):
+        return np.array_equal(u, v)
+
+    c, c0 = (
+        layered.coefficients([x], [m], derivatives=True),
+        mie.coefficients(x, m, derivatives=True),
+    )
+    assert same(c.a, c0.a) and same(c.b, c0.b)
+    assert same(c.d("layer0.x").a, c0.d("x").a) and same(c.d("layer0.m").b, c0.d("m").b)
+    q, q0 = (
+        layered.efficiencies([x], [m], derivatives=True),
+        mie.efficiencies(x, m, derivatives=True),
+    )
+    assert same(q.q_abs, q0.q_abs) and same(q.d("layer0.m_imag").q_abs, q0.d("m_imag").q_abs)
+    s, s0 = layered.amplitudes([x], [m], MU), mie.amplitudes(x, m, MU)
+    assert same(s.s1, s0.s1) and same(s.s2, s0.s2)
+    g, g0 = layered.greek([x], [m]), mie.greek(x, m)
+    assert same(g.alpha1, g0.alpha1) and same(g.beta2, g0.beta2)
+
+
+def test_identical_layers_are_the_sphere_bitwise():
+    q = layered.efficiencies([2.0, 5.0], [1.5 + 0.01j] * 2)
+    q0 = mie.efficiencies(5.0, 1.5 + 0.01j)
+    assert np.array_equal(q.q_ext, q0.q_ext) and np.array_equal(q.g, q0.g)
+
+
+def test_derivatives_match_central_differences():
+    x, m = np.array([1.0, 2.5]), np.array([1.95 + 0.79j, 1.43 + 0.01j])
+    q = layered.efficiencies(x, m, derivatives=True)
+    h = 1e-6
+    for layer in range(2):
+        for part in ("x", "m_real", "m_imag"):
+            xp, xm, mp, mm = x.copy(), x.copy(), m.copy(), m.copy()
+            if part == "x":
+                xp[layer] += h
+                xm[layer] -= h
+            else:
+                step = h if part == "m_real" else 1j * h
+                mp[layer] += step
+                mm[layer] -= step
+            fd = (layered.efficiencies(xp, mp).q_ext - layered.efficiencies(xm, mm).q_ext) / (2 * h)
+            got = q.d(f"layer{layer}.{part}").q_ext
+            assert got == pytest.approx(fd, rel=1e-7, abs=1e-9), (layer, part)
+
+
+def test_complex_derivatives_and_selected_names():
+    c = layered.coefficients(X, M, derivatives=["layer1.m_real"])
+    assert c.params == ("layer1.m_real",)
+    np.testing.assert_array_equal(c.d("layer1.m").a, c.d("layer1.m_real").a)
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="no derivative"):
+        c.d("layer0.x")
+    s = layered.amplitudes(X, M, MU, derivatives=True)
+    np.testing.assert_array_equal(s.d("layer0.m_imag").s1, 1j * s.d("layer0.m").s1)
+    q = layered.efficiencies(X, M, derivatives=True)
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="real"):
+        q.d("layer0.m")
+    assert layered.parameters(2) == (
+        "layer0.x",
+        "layer0.m_real",
+        "layer0.m_imag",
+        "layer1.x",
+        "layer1.m_real",
+        "layer1.m_imag",
+    )
+
+
+def test_results_are_read_only():
+    q = layered.efficiencies(X, M, derivatives=True)
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
+        q.q_ext[0] = 1.0
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
+        q.d("layer0.x").q_ext[0] = 1.0
+
+
+def test_errors():
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="trailing axis"):
+        layered.efficiencies(1.0, 1.5)
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="one value per layer"):
+        layered.efficiencies([1.0, 2.0], [1.5, 1.33, 1.2])
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="at least one layer"):
+        layered.efficiencies(np.zeros((2, 0)), np.zeros((2, 0)))
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unknown parameter"):
+        layered.efficiencies(X, M, derivatives=["x"])
+    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
+        layered.efficiencies(X, M, derivatives="layer0.x")
+    with pytest.raises(InvalidInputError, match="nondecreasing"):
+        layered.efficiencies([2.0, 1.0], [1.5, 1.33])
+    with pytest.raises(InvalidInputError, match="element 1"):
+        layered.efficiencies([[1.0, 2.0], [1.0, 2.0]], [[1.5, 1.33], [1.5, -1.0]])
+    with pytest.raises(OutsideValidatedDomainError):
+        layered.efficiencies([1.0, 2e4], [1.5, 1.33])
+    assert layered.efficiencies([1.0, 2e4], [1.5, 1.33], allow_unvalidated=True).q_ext > 0
+    # Derivatives are validated up to an outer size parameter of 100.
+    with pytest.raises(OutsideValidatedDomainError, match="derivatives"):
+        layered.efficiencies([50.0, 200.0], [1.5, 1.33], derivatives=True)
+    assert layered.efficiencies([50.0, 200.0], [1.5, 1.33]).q_ext > 0
+
+
+def test_default_n_max_is_the_spheres_at_the_outer_size():
+    np.testing.assert_array_equal(layered.default_n_max(X), mie.default_n_max(X[:, -1]))

index b7849f4..d9697ba 100644
--- a/tests/test_package.py
+++ b/tests/test_package.py
@@ -86,6 +86,7 @@ def test_test_hooks_live_outside_the_extension_namespace():
     assert set(_scatrs.__all__) == {
         "MAX_GAUSS_LEGENDRE_POINTS",
         "MAX_GREEK_ORDER",

         "MAX_MIE_VALUES",

@@ -94,6 +95,11 @@ def test_test_hooks_live_outside_the_extension_namespace():
